# Ethiopian Synaxarium: Miyazya (Mois de miyāzyā)

**Author**: Anonymous (Ethiopian Synaxarion)  
**Series**: PO Tome 46  
**Columns**: pp. 486–596  

[p. 486] {#po.46.p486}
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። ወርኃ ፡ ሚያዝያ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ መዓልቱ ፡ ፲ወ፻! ወእምዝ ፤ ይትዌሰክ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. The month of miyäzyä (is) blessed. Its (first) day (is) of thirteen (hours), then (the days) increase.

### READING OF THE FIRST OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፣ ስልዋኖስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ብፁዕ ፣ መንኩሰ ፡ እምንእሱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወተጋደለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸባብ ፡ ወተጸምዶደ ፡ በጾም ፡ ነዊኅ ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ወትጋህ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወትሕትና ፡ ወፍቅር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ 'አበ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወኮነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይከ ሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ራእያተ ፡ መለኮታዊተ ፡ ወያየድዖ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ መንክራተ ። ወዝክቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ ተመስጠ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወወድቀ ፡ በገጹ ፡ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ነዊኃ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ይንግሮሙ ፡ዘበጽሐ ፡ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወኢፈቀደ ፡ ይንግሮሙ ፡ አላ ፡ አርመመ ፡ ወኮነ፣ ይበኪ ፡ ብካየ ፡ መሪረ ። ወሶበ ፡ አገበርዎ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቆሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሊተ ፡ መሰጡኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ወርኢኩ ፡ መኃደሪሆሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡፣ ወመካናተ ፡ ኩነኔ ። ወርኢክዎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፣፤ ብዙ ኃን ፡ ይወስድዎሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገሃነም ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሕዝባዊያን፤ ፡ ይወስድዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፤ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወለምንት ፣ ኢይበኪ፤!፤ ፡ አነ ፣ ለነፍስየ ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ከደነ ፡ ገጾ ፡ በቆብዑ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይፈቅድ ፣ እርአይ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኀላፊ ። ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ይወስዱኒ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጽሙድ ፣፤ ወመስተጋድል ፡ በግብር ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ወሥጋዊ ። ወኮነ፤ ፡ ይኤዝዞሙ፤ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያጽርዑ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ወየሀቡ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ እምሲሳዮሙ ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኅኀበ፤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ሀካይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ወለአርድእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ይቤሎ ፣ ለአረጋዊ ፡ ወለአርዳኢሁ ፡ "ተገበሩ ፡ አኮ፤ ፡ ለመብልዕ ፡ ኃላፊ ፡ አላ፤ ፡ ለመብልፅ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ኀርየት ፡ ላቲ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዘኢየኀይድዋ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዖ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ስልዋኖስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሀቦ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፤ ለዝንቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንብብ ፡ ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓት?” ፡ ወዕፁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ኖኅታ፣ ወኢትኅድግ ፡ በኅቤሁ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘይበልዕ?፤" ። ወገብረ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡
On this day died the holy father abbä Selwänos (= Silvanus). This blessed one became a monk from his childhood with the holy father abbä Magäres (= Macarius) in the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). He fought (spiritually) in all the narrow ways and devoted himself to long fasts, (to) prayer, (to) numerous vigils, (to) humility, and (to) charity; he became an eminent father. God revealed to him divine visions and made him know prodigious actions. (There is) this one: one day, his heart was taken away, he fell to the ground on his face and remained (thus) a long moment. Then he raised his head and the brothers who were with him asked him to tell them what had happened to him. He did not want to tell (it) to them but remained silent, and he shed bitter tears. When they had pressed him and had asked him to inform them of what had happened to him, he said to them: "I was taken away (and brought) to the Garden of joy and I saw the dwellings of the righteous and the places of punishment. I saw many monks (whom) they were leading into hell and many laymen (whom) they were leading into the kingdom of heaven. Why would I not weep, I, for my soul?" From that day he covered his face with his cowl; he said: "I do not want to see the light of this transitory world: after that, one would lead me toward the eternal darkness." This saint was devout and fought in spiritual and physical activity. He prescribed to his disciples not to interrupt the work of their hands and to give alms with what remained of their food. One day came to this saint a lazy monk. When he saw this holy old man and his disciples working with their hands, he said to the old man and to his disciples: "Work not for a transitory food but for a food that remains for eternal life for it is written in the holy Gospel that Märyäm (= Mary) chose for herself the good part that will not be taken from her." When he heard him speak thus, the old man abbä Selwänos gave an order to his disciple and said to him: "Give a book to this brother so that he may read, make him enter a cell, close the door of it upon him, and do not

leave anything with him that he (can) eat." The disciple did as the old man had commanded him. When the moment of the ninth hour arrived, the old man and his disciples prayed and ate their food, they did not call the monk. When the moment of dinner had passed, the monk looked with his eyes toward the door and waited for them to call him. When hunger consumed him, he came out of the cell, went to the holy abbä Selwänos, and said to him: "Did the brothers not eat their food today, my father?" The saint answered him and said to him: "Yes, they ate." (The monk) said to him: "Why did you not call me?" The saint answered him and said to him: "You, you are a spiritual man, you do not need bodily food, for you have chosen for yourself the good part. But we, bodily men, need a bodily food; because of that, we work with our hands." The monk knew that he had faulted by his word. He prostrated himself before the saint and said to him: "Forgive me, my father, for I have faulted." The saint Selwänos answered him and said to him: "My son, we need to toil as Märta (= Martha) toiled, for Märyäm was praised thanks to Märtä." The monk was corrected by the teaching of this father; he was to work continually with his hands and to give alms to the poor from what remained of it (= of his work).
ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወተግሣጻተ ፡ በፉዔት" ፡ በእንተ ፣ ተጋድሎ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ በርሥዕና ፡ ሠናይት" ፡ አይድዖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ። ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ቅሩባን፤" ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ሰአሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዘከርዎ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ጸሎቶሙ ። ወእሙንቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዘከሮሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወተአምጥሙ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ አረብ ፡ አረማውያን ፡ ዘላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፣ ወአገትዎሙ ፡ለአድባራተ -፡፡'ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወለቤተ ፡ክርስቲያነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ መቃርስ ፡ ወማኅረኩ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አድባራት ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወተንበሉ ፡ በአበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወሰደዶሙ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አረማዊያን ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወተመውኡ ፡ ወጐኾዩ ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ መኑሂ ፡ ዘይሰድ ዶሙ ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአእኩትዎ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡
This saint composed many homilies and useful exhortations on the subject of the spiritual combat. When he had finished his days in a beautiful old age, God the Most High made him know the moment of his death. He called the monks who (were) near him and was blessed by them; he asked them to remember him at the moment of their prayer. They in return asked him to remember them before God. He took leave of them and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day also, the pagan Arabs of Upper Egypt rose, invested the monasteries of the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis) and the church of the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius), and pillaged all the goods that (were) in all the monasteries. The monks gathered, prayed, and asked for the intercession of the holy fathers. Our Lord Christ chased the pagans immediately; they were defeated and fled, but there was no one who chased them, except the power of our Lord Jesus Christ. The monks gave thanks to God for his clemency toward them—glory to him in the ages of ages, amen!
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ግጻዌ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውስተ ፡፤ መጽሐፈ ፡ ግጸዌ ፡ ዘላዕላይ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዓለ፤ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአሮን ፡ ካህን ፤ እጉሁ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ በኦሪት ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አመ ፡ ሣልስት ፡ ዕለት ፡ በዳግማይ ፡ ወርኅ ፡
On this day also, according to what is written in the lectionary of the city of Alexandria and in the lectionary of Upper Egypt, there is the commemoration of the feast of the death of 'Aron (= Aaron) the priest, brother of Musë (= Moses) the prophet. What is written in the Law says that he died on the third


[p. 490] {#po.46.p490}
በጸአቶሙ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፤ እምግብጽ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ሰሙኑ፣ ለወርኃ ፡ ግንቦት ። አላ ፤ አውራኃተ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ የዐውዱ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዓመት ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለወርኃ ፡ ሚያዝያ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እሁ ፡ ለሙሴ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እንባራም፤ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወመምህረ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ እሃ ፣፡ ለምርያም ፡ ነቢያዊት ፡ ወኮኑ" ፡ እምነገደ ፡ ሌዊ ። ወገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡፣ እደዊሁ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወኅረዮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡፤ ወለደቂቁ ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወወሀቦቶ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ንዋየ፥0፣ ደቂቀ ፤ እስራኤል ፡ ወእመሥዋዕቲሆሙተ" ። ወሶበ ፡ ተንሥኡ፥፤ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ቆሬ ፡ አጥፍኦሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዘዛ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወከሠተት ፡ አፉሃ ፡ ወውሕጠቶሙ ። ወሶበ= ፡ አሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ፡ በገድሉ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወዓቀበ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወፈለሰ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ አሮን ፡ ካህን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
day of the second month of the exodus of the sons of Israel from Egypt; this took place on the eighth of the month of genbot. But the months of the Jews rotate and, that year, it took place on the first of the month of miyäzyä. This righteous man was the brother of Musë the prophet, the son of 'Enbäräm (= Amram) the chief of the prophets and teacher of the Law, the brother of Märyäm (= Miriam) the prophetess. They were of the tribe of Lëwi (= Levi). God performed by his hands numerous miracles in the land of Egypt. God chose him, him and his children, and made them his priests. He gave him the tithe of all the goods of the children of Israel and of their offerings. When the sons of Qorë (= Korah) rose (against him), God annihilated them and gave an order to the earth; (the latter) opened its mouth and swallowed them. When it had pleased God the Most High by his beautiful (spiritual) combat and had kept the Law, he went to God to whom (belongs) the glory forever. May the prayer and the blessing of this prophet 'Aron the priest be with us in the ages of ages, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፣ መጥሮና ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ዮስጦስ ፡ ወብእሲቱ ፡እለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕታተ ፡ምስለ ፡ አበክረዙን ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also died the holy Matronä (= Matrona), and the saint Yostos (= Justus) and his wife who became martyrs with 'Abakerazun. May their holy blessing be with their dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages, amen.]

### READING OF THE 2 MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፪ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡፤ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አፅረፈ ፡ አባ ፤ ስምዖን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ ሐለብ! ። እግዚአብሔር ፤ ይም ሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ወለፍቅርቱሂ ፡ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. [On this day died abbä Sem'on (= Simon) of the city of Halab. May God have mercy through his prayer on his dear 'Atnätëwos and also on his dear Kotolikäwit in the ages of ages, amen.]
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ክርስትፎሮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ከልብ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ በለዓተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወከለባት ። ወጹወውዎ" ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጸብእ ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ አምነ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለማቲያስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፄወውዎ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ የአምር ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ፄወውዎ ። ወአስተብቀዓ ፡ ኅበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ ወተናገረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ፄወውዎ ። ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ያመነድብዎሙ ፡ ለሕ ዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወቀሠፎ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ዘለትዕይንት ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አኮ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ ለእግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ታሥተዓግሰኒ ፡ ወትእ ኅዝ ፡ ኀይልየ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢኮንከ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወሰራዊትከ ፡ ትድኅኑ ፡ እምኔየ ። ወፈነወ ፣ መኩንን ፡ኅበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ፣፣
On this day, there is the holy Kresteforos (= Christopher) the martyr whose face was the face of a dog. This saint was from the land of the eaters of men and dogs. He was made prisoner in war. His father had believed through the intermediary of Mätiyäs (= Matthew) the apostle. When he was made prisoner, he did not know the language of the people who had made him prisoner. He supplicated God—glory to him!—, (God) loosened his tongue and he spoke in the language of the people who had made him prisoner. He reprimanded those who tormented the people of the Christians. The officer who (commanded) the army inflicted a great flogging upon him. The saint said to him: "If the commandment of my Lord Jesus Christ did not contain me and did not restrain my strength, you nor your soldiers would be saved from my (hand)." The officer dispatched to the emperor concerning him


[p. 492] {#po.46.p492}
ንጉሥ ፡ ፪፻ ሐራ ፡ከመ ፡ ያምጽእዎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ዘእንበለ፣ ግግፅና ። ወሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ በትር ፡ ወጸለየ" ፡ ወሠረጸት ፡ ወጸገየት ። ወሶበ ፡ ኃጥኡ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወበዝኃ ፡፣ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ኅብስት ። ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወተጠምቀ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ አንጾኪያ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ዳኪዮስ!" ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አፍርሆ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቲጦ ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፪አንስተ ፡ ዘማዊያተ ፡ ዘሠናይ ፡ አርአያሆን ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሐለየ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ እማንቱ ፡ አንስት ፡ ይክላ ፡ ያውድቃሁ፤ "ምስሌሆን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኃጢአት፤! ። ወገሠጾን ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአምና ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በእዴሁ?" ፡ ወነስሓ ፡ ወኮና ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወከማሁ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፪፻ሐራ ፡ ተአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ፤ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ አርእስቲ ሆሙ፤ ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወረገሞ ፡ ቅዱስ ፤ ለንጉሥ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦተወካፌ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወማኅደሩ ። ወተቄጥዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቴገን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወያንድዱ ፡ መትሕቴሁ! ። ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወኢበጽሖ ፡ ወኢምንትኒ ፡ እሙስና'" ። አላ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይምሕ ሮሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቴገን ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትናገር ፤ ወይሜህሮሙ፤! ፡ ለሰብእ ፤ ወውእቱ፤ ፡ ጥዑይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ፣ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወአምኑ ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወቀርቡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ቴገን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወአዘዘ ፣ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትርዎሙ" ፡ በአስይፍት ፤ ወመተርዎሙ ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ክርስቶፎሮስ"፤ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ" ። ወአውፅኦ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ እምውስተ ፡ ግብ ፡ ጥዑየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሙስና ። ወሶበ ፡ ደክመ" ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፣ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
and (had) him told what it was about him. The emperor sent him two hundred soldiers to bring him to him. He came with them of his own free will without fear. There was in his hand a staff, he prayed and (the staff) sprouted and bore fruit. When the soldiers lacked bread, the saint prayed and the bread was in abundance for them. They were very astonished and believed in our Lord Jesus Christ. When they arrived at the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), (the saint) was baptized with the Christian baptism by the hand of abbä Päwlos (= Paul) patriarch of the city of 'Ansokiyä. When he arrived before Däkiyos (= Decius) the emperor, he frightened him and (the latter) wanted to seduce him. He sent to him two prostitutes whose appearance (was) very beautiful. The emperor thought that these women could make him fall with them into sin. The saint reprimanded them, they believed in our Lord Christ through his intermediary, repented, and became martyrs. Likewise, the two hundred soldiers confessed our Lord Christ before the emperor. (The latter) ordered to cut off their heads with swords and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. The saint cursed the emperor and said to him: "O you who accept the work of Satan and (who are) his dwelling!" The emperor flew into a rage, ordered to throw him into a large cauldron and to light (a fire) under it. They did that to him, but no damage reached him, on the contrary he taught the people while he was in the cauldron. When the crowd saw him speak and teach the people (while) he was unharmed, without damage, it was very astonished, believed in our Lord Christ, and approached the cauldron to bring the saint out of it. The emperor ordered to cut them (= the people) up with swords, they were cut up with swords and they received the crown of martyrdom. After that the emperor ordered to hang a large stone from the neck of the saint Krestoforos and to throw him into a pit. They did to him as (the emperor) had ordered. The angel of God brought him out of the pit unharmed, without damage. When the emperor was weary of torturing him, he ordered to cut off his head; they cut off his head with a sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us forever, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ተዝካሩ ፣ ለመላልኤል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቃይናን ። ወሐይወ ፡ መላልኤል ፡፤ ፻፳ወ-፳ዓመተ ፡ ወወለዶ ፡ ለያሬድ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ቿ፻3ወ፳ዓመት ፡፤ ወሞተ ፡ መላልኤል ፡ በዕለተ ፡ እድ ፡ አመ ፡ ፪ለሚያዝያ ፡ ወተቀ በረ ፡፤ ውስተ ፡ በዐተ ፡ መዛግብት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ዳኪዮስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Maläl'ël (= Mahalalel), son of Qäynän (= Kenan). Maläl'ël lived one hundred and sixty-five years and begot Yärëd (= Jared). All his days were eight hundred and ninety-five years. Maläl'ël died on a Sunday, the 2nd of miyäzyä, and he was buried in the Cave of Treasures. May God have mercy through his prayer on his dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages, amen.] [On this day also died abbä Däkiyos (= Decius). May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages, amen.]

### READING OF THE 3 MIYAZYA

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father abbä Yohannes (= John), bishop of the city of Jerusalem. The parents of this saint were Jews and observed the law of the Torah. When this saint had grown up, his parents gave him the teaching of the law of the Torah. He became learned in it. He disputed with the Christians and fought them until [it was assured in his heart—and he believed (it)—that our Lord Christ had come into the world and was the true God. He believed through the intermediary of the saint abbä Yestos (= Justus), bishop of the city of Jerusalem; (the latter) baptized him with the Christian baptism and ordained him in the city of Jerusalem. Then he grew in the exercise of good and (in) science so much that he was worthy to be consecrated bishop of the city of Jerusalem. When 'Adreyänos (= Hadrian) reigned who is called 'Élyäs (= Aelius), (the latter) ordered to (re)build what had been destroyed of the city of Jerusalem and (ordered also that it) be called by his name 'Élyäs. Then he built on the gate of the temple of the Jews a tower, he put on its gate a tablet of precious stone and wrote on it his name "Élyäs". In his time, Jerusalem was filled with pagans and Jews. When the pagans saw the Christians coming to Golgotä (= Golgotha) and praying (there), they forbade them (to do so), built there a temple in the name of the star Venus, and forbade the Christians to pass in that place. Great sorrows, misfortunes, and trials arrived to this saint because of the pagans who lived in the city of Jerusalem. They struck him many times, dragged him (on the ground), and mistreated him. He asked God to receive his soul to him and died in peace after remaining on his seat two years. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ አባ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፤ እምኖልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ድወ፳ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ፈተወት ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ሠናይት፤" ፡ በአልባሰ! ፡ መላእክት ፡ ቅድስት፤" ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወመንኩሰ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያኑ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ በገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ። ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አረጋዌ ፤ ወተሠይመ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ በኩሉ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ሠናየ ፡ ወአሥመሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወጻጸመወ ፡ ጸማ ፡ ዐቢየ፡ ዘአብጽሖ፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለሚመተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሥርዓት ፡ መሲሓዊት ፡ ወበዓለም ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ጸጋ! ፡ ሰማያዊት፤፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘእምቅድሜሁ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌ
On this day also died the holy father, virgin (and) pure abbä Mikä'el (= Michael), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; in the number of the patriarch fathers, he is the seventy-first. The beautiful soul of this father desired the holy garment of the angels which is the monastic garment. He became a monk in the church of the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius) in the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). He remained in the desert until he became old, and he was named at the head of many monks. He led his whole life in a beautiful (spiritual) combat and pleased God. He provided great works that brought him, in this world, to the office of the patriarchate—which is the Christian office—and, in the world to come, (attracted to him) the heavenly grace. When died abbä Gabr'ël (= Gabriel), the patriarch who (was it) before him, the seat of Märqos (= Mark) the evangelist remained without a patriarch four months. and the doctors remained three months searching and choosing who would be the best for this office. After many searches and much effort, they chose three men of whom it was attested that they were of right faith and learned. They wrote their names on three small papers, wrote the name of our Lord Jesus Christ on an (other) paper, sealed each of them with wax, and placed them on the altar. The bishops, the priests, and the monks remained three days and three nights praying, offering (the Sacrifice), and supplicating God the Most High so that he might place over them a virtuous, priestly, and believing pastor. After three days, they called a small child and said to him "Draw for us one of these four (papers sealed with) wax." (The child) drew (the paper sealed with) wax where (was written) the name of this saint abbä Mikä'ël. All the people knew that God had chosen him; they all cried out saying: "He is worthy, he is worthy, he is worthy", and named him patriarch immediately. He observed in his office all beautiful conduct as is fitting in the patriarchate. He established for himself a scribe to write for him all the letters to all the cities and to the bishops. This father taught the people and reprimanded the sinners so that they might repent of their sins. All (his) flock feared him, for they knew well that God (was) with him. His soul was pure and he desired nothing of the sweetness of this world, neither its honors, nor its goods. He carried his strength and his attention to the poor and the needy and gave them what they needed, and what remained to him, he gave for the construction of churches. This father did not spend a full year in his office but less than that and he died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ክርስቲያናት ። ወኢፈጸመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በሚመቱ ፡ ዓመተ ፡ ፍጽምተ ፡ አላ ፡ ዘይንእስ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ መርቄ ፡ ነጋዲ ፡ ክርስቶሳዊ?፥ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ ወሀለዎ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወምእመን ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ጉስፋር፥" ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወመምለኬ ፡ ጣዖት ፡ ወኢይትፈለጡ ፡ በምንትኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በሃይማኖት ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ እመዋዕል ፡ ሖሩ ፡ መርቄ ፡ ወጉስፋር" ፡ ለነጊድ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ በግዳድ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ፳ዕለተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ፥ ፡ በገዳም ፡ ዘአልቦቱ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወመርቄ ፡ ክርስቲያናዌ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ቋልጥረ ፡ ወርቅ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ደወየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ፅኑዓ ፣ ዘያበጽሕ ፤ ለሞት ። ወእምዝ ፡ አስተጸንዓ ፡ ርእሶ ፣ ወጸሐፈ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ገብርከ ፡ መርቄ ፡ ወወልደ ፡ አመትከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብየ ፡ ፀልጥረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ጽዕንት ፡ በበቅል ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ትኩን ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ዘለከ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ህየንተ ፡ አበሳየ ፡ ወጌጋይየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀበኒ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡
[On this day also died Margë the Christian merchant. This saint was of the people of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch), he had a dear and faithful (friend) by the name of Gusfär, a pagan and worshiper of idols. They were separated by nothing if not by faith. One (day) among the days, Margë and Gusfär went to trade in the city of Bagdäd (= Baghdad). They remained five days traveling in a desert without men; Margë was a rich Christian and had with him thirty pounds of gold. While he was on the way, he suffered from a violent illness that brought him (near) to death. After that, he gathered his strength and wrote (a paper) saying: "From your servant Margë, the son of your handmaid: I have thirty pounds of gold loaded on a black mule; may they be for you, O my Lord Christ Son of God—you to whom (belongs) the glory—, in compensation

ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወቀሲስ ፡ ታዴዎስ ፡ አርኃወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አንቀጸ ። ወበዊኦ ፡ ጉስፋር፥" ፡ አንበረ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ወርቀ፣07፣ ላዕለ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ወወዩኦ ፡ አንቀጸ ፤ አፀወ ። ወቆመቶ" ፡ በህየ ፡ ወጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ድምፀ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፣ ውሣጤ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሠረቀ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዐቢይ ፤ ወግብተ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በስብሐት ፤ ዐቢይ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ፪መላእክት ፡ ወነበረ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መል አክ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትቀውም ፡ ዝየ ፡ ጉስፋር ፡ ከሓዲ ። ወነገሮ ፡ ጉስፋር ፡ ከመ ፡ አምሐሎ ፡ መርቄ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሀብ ፡ ለካልእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ነዓ ፡ ስግድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወሰገደ ፡ ጉስፋር፤ ፡ ወመጠዎ ፡ ክርታሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አመንኩ ፡ ብከ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘለከ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ስብሐት ። ወአዘዘ ፡ ይድልዎ ፡ ለወርቅ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ልጥረ ። ወአዘዞ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ለጉስፋር፤፤ ፡ ከመ ፤ ይጠመቅ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ታዴዎስ ፡ ወተጠምቀ ፡ ምስለ፣ ሹሉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ድወ፳ ነፍስ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በሃይማኖተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ መርቄ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
of my faults and of my errors, so that you may give me the kingdom of heaven. May they not be for my children nor for my wife nor for my family." He rolled up the paper and sealed it; he called Gusfär his friend and made him swear by his gods to do for him everything that he would tell him. When (Gusfär) had sworn, he said to him: "When I die, do not touch me, but take this paper and the thirty pounds with the mule, and give them to my Lord Jesus Christ, Son of God—glory to him! Hand (them) to him with your hand and do not give (them) to my children." Gusfär said to him: "(Are you) crazy, my friend? Do you not say that Christ is dead, is resurrected, and has ascended into the heavens? Can I ascend to the heavens to give (them) to him?" Margë said to him: "Go to the church and he himself will come to you, give (them) to him in his own hands." After that, when (Margë) was near to dying, Gusfär sat aside waiting for his death. He saw angels descend to him (= Margë) holding a garment of light; with them (there were) the righteous and the martyrs, Däwit (= David) was psalmodying with a harp and the Holy Spirit descended under the appearance of a white dove and turned three times above his body. When the holy soul of Margë saw (this), it went out of his body and ascended to heaven in great glory. After that came two lions, they dug his (= Margë's) grave and buried him. As for Gusfär, he loaded the gold and went away wondering at everything he had seen. When he was near 'Ansokiyä, a vision told Tädëwos (= Thaddaeus), the steward of the church, to receive Gusfär the pagan and to ask him for the gold with the mule. When the priest told him that, Gusfär said to him: "I, I will only give (them) to Jesus Christ—glory to him!—in his own hands, as my friend made me swear." And, leading the mule, he went to the church and the priest Tädëwos opened the door for him. (Once) inside, Gusfär placed the gold on the altar and closed the door while leaving. He stood there and, at the moment of midnight, he heard the noise of thunder inside the church. A great light shone and, suddenly, our Lord Jesus Christ descended from heaven in great glory: (there were) two angels with him and he sat on the altar. An angel said to him (= to Gusfär): "Why do you stand here, Gusfär the pagan?" Gusfär told him that Margë had made him swear concerning the gold not to give (it) to anyone other than Jesus Christ—glory to him! The angel said to him: "Come, prostrate yourself, for this one is Christ, Son of God, to whom (belongs) the glory." Gusfär prostrated himself and handed (the paper) to him (= to Christ) saying: "I believe in you, my Lord Jesus Christ, you to whom belongs the glory." (Christ) ordered to weigh the gold, and (the latter) made thirty pounds. Our Lord ordered Gusfär to be baptized by the priest Tädëwos, he was baptized with all the people of his house—seventy-five souls—and died in the faith of Christ—glory to him! May the prayer of this saint Margë be with his dear 'Atnätëwos in the ages of ages, amen.]

### READING OF THE 4 MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፪ለሚያገዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ፊቅጦር ፡ ወዳኪዮስ፤ ፡ ወኤሬኒ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዕድ ፡ ወአንስት ፡ ወደናግል ። እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለቄስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወበመዋዕለ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ አንሐሉ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ጣዖታት ፤ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወሰበሩ ፤ ጣዖታቲሆሙ ፡ ወአውዓይዎሙ ፡ በእሳት ። ወሐነጹ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፤ ጣዖታት" ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ አብያተ ፡፤ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወመሥዋዕተ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወበስሞሙ ፡ ለቅዱ ሳን ። ወሶበ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ቁስጠንጢኖስ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ሉልያኖስቶ ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወአቀመ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወአክበሮሙ ፡ ለገነውቶሙ ፡ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሆሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ በአብያተ ፡ ጣዖታት ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ሰበሩ ፡ ጣዖታተ ። 'ወአኃዝዎሙ ፡ ወኩነንዎሙ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወቀሠፍዎሙ ፡ ወሰቀልዎሙ ፡ ወሰጠቁ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡ በመጥባሕታት ፡ ዘኀጺን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መተሩ ፡ አርእስቲ ሆሙ ፡ በአስይፍት ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, there are the holy martyrs Figtor (= Victor), Däkiyos (= Decius), ‘Éreni (= Irene), and many people—men, women, and virgins. In the time of the reign of Quastantinos (= Constantine) and in the time of his son, these saints had destroyed many temples, had broken their idols, and had burned them with fire. They had built in the temples many churches and altars in the name of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, mother of God, and in the name of the saints. When died Quastantinos (= Constantius), Lulyänos (= Julian) the apostate reigned; he restored the worship of idols, built temples, honored their priests, and killed many Christians. He learned the story of these saints, how they had acted with the temples and how they had broken the idols. They were seized and were inflicted with great torture for many days. They were flogged, they were suspended, and their bodies were torn with iron knives. After that, their heads were cut off with swords and they received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also died the holy father abbä Yeshaq (= Isaac) the abbot. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING OF THE 5 MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቡዚ፤ ፡ ካህን ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ" ፡ ኮነ ፡ ካህነ ፡ ወነቢየ ፡ ወተፄወወ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፄወዎሙ ፡ ናቡክድናጸር ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ። ወወረደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፣ መንፈሰ ፡ ትን ቢት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወተናገረ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ቃላተ ፡ መንክራተ ። ወይቤ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ « ክርስቶስ ፡ እምነ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ወለደቶ ፡ ነበረት ፡ በድንግልናሃ ። ወተነበየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፤ ከመዝ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ርኢኩ ፡፤ በምሥራቅ ፡ ፕኅተ ፡ ዕፁተ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ጥፕኅት ፡ ትነብር ፡ ፅፁታ ፡ ወኢይትረኃው ፡ ወኢይበውእ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኢይወልእ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አምላከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይበውእ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ እምኔሃ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ዘትቄድስ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the great prophet, the righteous Hezge'ël (= Ezekiel), son of Buzi (= Buzi) the priest. This righteous man was a priest and prophet, he was taken captive to the city of Bäbilon (= Babylon) when Näbukednäsar (= Nebuchadnezzar) took captive the children of Israel. The spirit of prophecy descended upon him and, while he was in the land of Bäbilon, he uttered in his prophecy prodigious words. He spoke in his prophecy of the birth of our Lord Jesus Christ from our Lady the holy Virgin Mary: after having given birth to him, she remained in her virginity. He prophesied by speaking thus: "I saw at the east a closed gate. The Lord said to me: This gate shall remain closed, it shall not be opened; only the Lord, God of Israel, shall enter and go out of it; he shall enter it and go out of it." He prophesied on the Christian baptism that sanctifies the soul of man and his body, tears from him the heart of stone, and makes him a son of God by the descent of the Holy Spirit upon him. He reprimanded


[p. 502] {#po.46.p502}
ወሥጋሁ ፡ ወትመልሕ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ልበ ፡ዘእብን ፡ ወትሬስዮ" ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ በርደተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ላዕሌሁቾ ። ወገሠጾሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀዲጎቶሙ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ተዓቀቡ ፡ እምሀኬት ፡ ወኢትትዓወሩ ፡ 'እምነ ፡ ሕዝብ!" ። 'ወከሠተ ፡ ሎሙ፤፤ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ የኃሥሥ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፤ ሕዝብ!" ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢመሀርክምዎሙ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኢያንቃህክም ዎሙ ። ወተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፤ ትንሣኤ፤ ፡ ወከሠተ ፤ ከመ ፡ አብድንት ፡ ሀለዎሙ ፡ ይትነሥኡ ፡ ወይከውኑ፤ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ነፍሳቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወይነሥኡ!፤፤ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይደልዎሙ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ትፍሥሕተ ። ወተነበየ ፡ ትንቢታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ዘትበቅዕ ፡ ባቲ" ፡ ለዘ ፡ ያነብብ ። ወከሠተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፤ እደዊሁ ፤ ተኣምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ። ወሶበ ፡ አምለኩ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡፣ እስራኤል ፡ ለጣዖታት ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ባቢሎን ፡ ወገሠጾሙ ፡ በእንተዝ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ሊቀውንቲሆሙ ፡ ወቀተልዎ ። ወተነበየ ፤ እምቅድመ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡
the priests because they were abandoning the teaching of the people, and he said to them: "Guard yourselves against sloth and do not turn your eyes away from the people." He made a revelation to them and said: "God will demand from you the souls of the people if you do not teach them and if you do not awaken them." He prophesied about the resurrection and revealed that the bodies would rise, would become one with their souls as they were before, and would receive according to what they deserved: either punishment or joy. He made many prophecies [which are useful to the one who has read them]. God caused many miracles to be seen through his hands. When the children of Israel worshipped idols in the city of Babylon, he rebuked them because of this. Their leaders rose up against him and killed him. He prophesied five hundred and seventy-seven years before the incarnation of our Lord Christ, and the days of his prophecy were twenty years. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ክርስቶስ ፡ በ ፳፻ ዓመት ፡ ድ ወወኮነ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ፳ዓመት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ገድሉ ፡ ለአፍላሚስ ፡ ወታኦድራ ፡ ወአርሳኒ ፡ መነኮስ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also, (there is) the (spiritual) combat of 'Aflämis, Tä'odrä (= Theodora), and 'Arsäni (= Arsenius) the monk. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear 'Atnätéwos for ever and ever.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ዋሲልድስ፤ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ።
[On this day also died abbä Waäsildes (= Basilides?). May his prayer and his blessing be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever.]

### READING FOR THE 6TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ሄለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፡ አፅረፈት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ገዳማዊት፤ ፡ ማርያም ፡፣ ግብጻዊት ። ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኮነት ፤ እምብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሃ ፡ ክርስቲያን" ። ወጌጣ" ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ወአስሐታ ፡ ወረሰያ ፡ መሥዝረርተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወአስገረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ ዘኢይትጐለቀ ። ወውእ ቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ረሰያ ፡ ወወሀበት ፡ ሥጋሃ ፡ ለዝሙት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አስብ ፡ አላ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅራ ፡ ለኃጢአት ፡ ጠፋኢት ። ወነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ርኩስ ፡መጠነ ፡፲ ወጻዓመት ፡ ወፍቅራ ፡ ይትዌሰክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ርኩስ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ ፍቅረ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ አርአያሃ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ እንዘ፤ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ ወይትባረኩ ፡ እመቃብሪሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy anchoress Märyäm (= Mary) the Egyptian. This saint was from the land of Egypt, from the city of Alexandria; her parents were Christians. The enemy of the human race seduced her, deceived her, made her a net for himself, and captured through her many souls, innumerable. This (happened) thus: Satan made her give her body to fornication without pay, but for the love of destructive sin. She remained in this impure way of acting for seventeen years, and her love (of fornication) increased in this impure way of acting. By the will of God's love for the human race, (God) made her see people wanting to go—and to be blessed by the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ—to the city of Jerusalem. She stirred in her heart the thought of going with them, and she boarded a boat with

many people. When they began to move forward, the sailors said to her: "Give us the price of the (boat trip)." As she had no money to give them, she gave them her body to fornicate with in place of the price of the (boat trip). When she arrived at the city of Jerusalem, she acted in this way and she fornicatied continually. When she wanted to enter through the door of the church of the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ, the divine power prevented her from entering. When she wanted to enter with the people who were entering, the power of God prevented her from entering. When she had acted thus many times and had not succeeded in entering, she thought that (the power of God) had prevented her from entering because of her impure way of acting. She lifted her eyes toward God the merciful, grieving and sorrowing in her heart, and she saw the image of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. She wept before her and implored her, saying: "O you who intercede for all the people of the Christians, O Mother of God, grant me credit: if I enter and celebrate the feast entirely like all the people, I will do everything that you command me." When she had said this while weeping, she entered quickly into the church of the tomb of our Lord Jesus Christ. When she had finished the ceremonies of the feast, she returned near the image of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, made a long prayer before her, shed bitter tears, and begged her to send her to the place she would want so that she might save her soul. A voice came to her from the image of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, saying to her: "If you enter the desert of Yordänos (= Jordan), you will find rest and salvation." She accepted this word, prayed, asked for the intercession of our Lady Mary, and departed.
ቦእኪ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ትረክቢ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ ወመድኃኒተ ። ወተወክፈት ፡ ውእተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወጸለየት ፡ ወተንበለት ፡ በሥዕለ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፤ ማርያም ፡፣ ወወፅአት ። ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ ረከበት ፡ ፳ብእሴ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ይጸራይቀ ፡ ወተሣየጠት ፡ ቦሙ ፣ ኅብስተ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ዐደወት ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ። ወነበረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ጓወዒዓመተ ፡እንዘ ፡ ትትጋደል ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ይፀብኣ ፡ በፍ ቅረ ፡ ርኩስና ፡ ዘነበረት ፡ ትገብር ፡ ቀዳሚ ። ወትሴሰየት፤ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ኅብስት ፣ ዘተሣየጠቶን ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ መዋዕለ ። ወኮነት ፤ ትጸውም ፡ በበ፪መዓልት ፡ ፪መዓልት ፡ ወበበ፻መዓልት ፡የይመዓልት ፡ወትሴሰይ ፡እምውእቱ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ በበንስቲት ፡ ንስቲት። ወሶበ ፡ ሐልቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትሴሰይ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ገዳም ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ጓወ፳ዓመት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተዐይል ፡ ውስተ ፡፣ ይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ዶጸመ ፡ዓቢየ፣ ዘጓመዓልት ፡ ቅድስት ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማደ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ለለዓመት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ጾም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጻኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይፀመዱ ፡ ወይትጋደሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ይትፌጸም፤ ፡ ጾም ። ወሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያርእዮ ፡ በዘይትናዘዝ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የአይል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ርእያ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እምርጐቅ ፣
When she departed, she found a man (who) gave her three small coins, and she bought bread with them. Then she crossed the river of Yordänos. She remained in the desert of Yordänos for forty-seven years, leading a great (spiritual) combat. Satan attacked her through the love of impurity that she practiced before. She fed herself for many days on the bread she had bought. She fasted two days in a row and three days in a row. She fed herself on this bread in small quantities. When the bread was finished, she fed herself on the grass of the desert. When she had spent forty-five years wandering in the desert, the holy priest Zosimäs (= Zosimus) went out into the desert of Yordänos to perform there the great fast of the forty holy days, according to the custom of his monastery: indeed, (it was) the custom of the monks of his monastery to go out each year into the desert for the great fast and to devote themselves (there) to devotion and asceticism until the fast was finished. When the holy Zosimäs went out into the desert of Yordänos, he asked God to let him see someone by whom he would be comforted. While he was wandering in the desert, he


[p. 506] {#po.46.p506}
ወመሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእቲ፤ ሰብእ ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወጐየት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይተልዋ ፡ ወይረውፅ ፡ ድኅሬሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ትጐይይ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ጸውዓቶ ፣ በስሙ ፡፣ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ኦዞሲማስ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ"፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትናገር ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ውግር ፡ ሊተ ፡ጸርቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትከደን ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ዕርቅት ። ወአንከረ፡፡ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጸውዓቶ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ወወገረ ፡ ላቲ ፡ በዘ ፡ ትትከደን ፡ ቦቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሰገደት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወሰገደ፤ ፡ ላቲ፤ ፡ ወተኣም ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ። ወሰአለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸሊ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ካህን ፡፣ ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጠይቆ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገድላ ። ወነገረቶ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ እምጥንቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ውእቱ" ፡ ጊዜ ።
saw this saint from afar, and it seemed to him that she was a demon. When he had prayed, it appeared to him that she was a human being. He went toward her, and she fled far from him; he followed her and ran behind her to reach her, she fled far from him. After that, she called him by his name, saying: "O Zosimäs, if you want to speak with me, throw me a piece of cloth so that I may cover myself, for I am naked." He was astonished when she called him by his name and threw her something to cover herself. When she came to him, she prostrated herself before him, he prostrated himself before her, and they embraced. She asked him to pray for her, for he was a priest, then he asked her—and he prostrated himself before her—to make known to him all her (spiritual) combat. She told him everything that had happened to her from the beginning until that moment.
ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ በዓመት ፣ ዘይመጽእ ፡፤ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ እወ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ፍጽምት ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽዋፅ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ በለስ ፡ ወተምር ፡ ወብርስን ፡ ዘርሑስ ፡ በማይ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሐይቀ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወርእያ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትመጽእ" ፡ ወታንሶሱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ተኣምጐ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወጸለዩ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ መጠዋ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወአውዕአ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ተምረ ፡ ወፍሬ ፡ በለስ ፡ ወብርስነ ፡ ወሰ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትመጠው ፡እምኔሁ። ወነሥአት ፡ በእዴሃ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እምብርስን ፡ በእንተ ፡ በረከት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሰአለቶ ፥ ከመ ፡ ይትመ የጥ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ በዳግም ፡ ዓመት ፡ ዘይመጽእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ካልእት ፡ ዓመት ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ወረከባ ፡፣ ለቅድስት ፡ በዘአዕረፈት ። ወንቡር፤ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘጽሑፍ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ከመዝ" ፡ ቅብራ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ነዳይት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መሬት ፡ ዘተፈጥረት ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወአንከረ ፡ እምጽሕፈት ፡ ወርእየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እገሪሃ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዓቅባ ። ወሶበ ፡፣ ሐለየ ፡ በልቡ፥ ፡ በምንት ፡ ይከርይ ፡ ላቲ ፡ መቃብረ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወከረየ ፡ ላቲ ፡ መቃብረ፥ ። ወቀበራ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብሩ ፡ ወዜነዎሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ገድላ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ማርያም ፡ ግብጻዊት ፡ እምጥንታ ፡ እስከ ፣ ተፍጻሜታ ፡ ወአንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር?" ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወታ ፡ ድ ወኒዷዓመት?፥ጾ ።
Then she asked him to return to her the following year and (to bring) with him the body and blood of Christ; he said yes to her. When a full year had passed, he took (a little) of the holy body and (of the) glorious blood in a glass and took (also) figs, dates, and lentils soaked in water. He came to the bank of the river of Yordänos and saw the saint coming and walking on the river of Yordänos. When she had arrived at him, they embraced and prayed; then he administered the holy Mysteries to her. He brought out for her the dates, the figs, and the lentils and asked her to receive (them) from him. She took a few lentils for the blessing. Then she asked him to return to her again the following year. When it was (that) other year, he went near the river of Yordänos and found the saint dead. Placed near her head (there was) a paper where it (was) written thus: "Bury Märyäm the wretched in the dust from which she was created." He was astonished at the inscription and saw near her (= Märyäm's) feet a lion guarding her. As he was reflecting in his heart on what he would dig a grave for her with, the lion came and dug a grave for her. The holy Zosimäs buried her, returned to his monastery, and told the monks the (spiritual) combat of this saint Märyäm the Egyptian from the beginning to the end. They were very astonished and praised God. All the days of her life were seventy-seven years. May God have mercy on us through her prayer, amen.
እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አስተርአዮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለቶ ማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ለትንሣኤሁ ። ወአርአዮቶ፥ ፡ አሰሮሙ ፡ ለቅንዋት ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወአንበራ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ መለኮታዊ ፡ መካነ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ዘረገዝዎ" ፡ ባቲ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርኢከኒ ፡ አመንከ፥፤ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይርአየኒ ። ወበትርጓሜ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቶማስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ውዕየ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ እምእሳተ ፡ መለኮት ።
On this day also, our Lord Christ—glory to him!—appeared to Tomäs (= Thomas) the apostle on the eighth (day) after his resurrection. He showed him the trace of the nails in his hands and his feet, he took his hand and put it into his divine side at the place (where) the lance (had struck) with which he had been pierced. Tomäs said to him: "My Lord and my God." Our Lord answered him and said to him: "When you saw me, you believed: blessed is he who believes in me without seeing me!" In a commentary on the Gospel, it is said that,


[p. 508] {#po.46.p508}
ወሶበ ፡ ተአምነ ፡ በመለኮቱ ፡ ተፈወሰ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ እምዋዕይ ። ወስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወኪያሁ ፡ ንስእል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሐረነ ፤ በጸሎታቲሆሙ ፡፥፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ሐዋርያቲሁ ፡ ወቅዱሳኒሁ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
when Tomäs the apostle put his hand into the side of our Lord, his hand burned with the fire of divinity. When he confessed his (= Jesus') divinity, his hand was healed of the burn. Glory to our Lord Jesus Christ! We ask him to have mercy on us through the prayer of all his apostles and his saints,
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዘዙነ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፣ ተዝካረ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለአ ቡነ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ሔዋን ። ወለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አዳም ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይፍጥሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ፈጠረ ፤ ሎቱ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘውስቴቶሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወሊቃነ ፡፤ መላእክት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተከለ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ኤዶም ፡ ዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ወዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ጣዕመ ፡ ፍሬሁ ። ወአስተናቢሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መፍቅደ ፡ ፈጠረ ፤ ኪያሁ ፡፤ ነሚኦ ፡ መሬተ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ጎልጎታ ፡ በጊዜ ፤ ክሰዓት ፡ ዘዐርብ ፡ ወገብሮ ፡ እምነ ፡ 8ጠባይዕ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡፥ ማይ ፡ ወእሳት ፡ ወነፋስ ፡ ወመሬት ፡ ወአስተማሰሎ ፡ በአርአያሁ ፡ ወአምሳሊሁ ። ወነፍሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወሴሞ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ወካህነ ፡ ወነቢየ ፡ ወመጋቤ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘፈጠረ ። ወእምዝ ፡፤ አምጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አራዊተ ፤ ወእንስሳተ ፡ ወአፅዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአዮሙ ፡ ወይሰሚ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ። ወሰመዮሙ ፡ አዳም ፡ በበአስማቲሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ አንበቦ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ትንቢት ፡ ዘላዕ ሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይግበር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቢጸ ፡ ዘይረድኦ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ድቃሰ ፣ ወኖመ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ 8አፅመ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ወመልአ ፡ ሥጋ ፣ መካና ፡፤ ወአንበራ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀ ፡ አዳም ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዐፅም ፡ እምዐፅምየ ፤ ወሥጋ ፡ እምሥጋየ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ለትኩነኒ ፤ ብእሲትየ ፡ ወሰመያ ፡ ስማ ፡ ሔዋን።
amen. [On this day, our fathers have prescribed for us to celebrate the commemoration of the death of our father 'Adäm (= Adam) and (of) our mother Hëwän (= Eve). Before creating this father 'Adäm, ours, God created for him the heavens, the earth, and everything that is (in them), including the angels and the archangels. Then he planted for him the garden of 'Édom (= Eden) whose aspects are diverse and diverse the taste of the fruits. Having arranged everything that was necessary, he created him (= 'Adäm) having taken dust from the middle of the earth, that is to say (from) Golgotä (= Golgotha), at the moment of the third hour of Friday; he made him with four elements which are water, fire, air, and dust, and he fashioned him in his image and his likeness. He breathed onto his face the breath of life, clothed him with garments of light, and named him king, priest, prophet, and administrator of everything he had created. Then he brought before him all the wild and domestic animals and the birds of the sky so that he might see them and give them names. 'Adäm named them each by its name, according to what the spirit of prophecy which (was) upon him had dictated (to him). When God wanted to make for him a companion who would help him, he caused a stupor to come upon him, and ('Adäm) fell asleep. Then God took a bone from his (= 'Adäm's) side, filled its (= the bone's) place with flesh, and placed her before him (= 'Adäm). When 'Adäm awoke, he prophesied and said: "Bone of my bone and flesh of my flesh! May this one be my wife." And he called her by the name of Hëwän
ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አዳም ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ጎልጎታ ፡ ወይሰምዕ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ጾረቶ ፡ ደመና ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሔዋን ፤ ብእሲቱ ፡፤ ወነሥአቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ኤዶም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሑ ፡ ቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ጾታ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኪሩቤል ፤ ወሱራፌል ፡ ወየአኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘአክበሮ ፡ ለአዳም ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ወለሔዋን ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ብልዑ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ እምዕፅ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ገነት ፤ ኢትብልዑ ፡ እስመ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ትበልዑ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ሞተ ፡፤ ትመውቱ ። ወነበረ ፡ አዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጌበራ ፡ ለገነት ፡ ወይበልዕ ፡ እምፍሬሃ ፡ ወይሴብሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መላእክት ። ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ዙሉ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ እመዐርጊሁ ፡ ወክብሩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትዕቢት፤ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ወቀንዓ ፡ በክብረ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወስብሐቲሁ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ሔዋን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ምድር ። ወአስፈጣ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብላዕ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ዘአኅረማ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአሰፈዋ ፡ ከዊነ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ አደማ ፡ ፍሬሁ ፡ በልዐት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወአብልዓቶ ፡ ለአዳም ። ወሰሰለ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወአእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕሩቃን ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወከደኑ ፡ ኃፍረቶሙ ፡ በቄጽለ ፡ በለስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስሕተቶሙ ፡ ረገማ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ለአርዌ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወአስተፃረረ ፡ ማእከ ሌሃ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ ዘርኣ ፡ ለሔዋን ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ ረገማ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በሕማም ፡ ለዲ ፡
While 'Adäm was standing in the place of Golgotä, he heard the voice of his God: behold, a cloud of light lifted him up with Hëwän his wife and carried them into the garden of 'Édom, the classes of angels, of cherubim, and of seraphim giving glory before them and praising God because he had honored 'Adäm. God said to 'Adäm and to Hëwän: "Eat of all the trees that are in the garden, but do not eat of the tree that is in the middle of the garden, for, the day you eat of it, you will surely die." 'Adäm remained cultivating the garden, eating of its fruits, and giving glory with the angels. When Satan, the enemy of all good, was fallen from his rank and his honors because of the pride he had shown, he envied the honors of 'Adäm and his glory: he went where Hëwän was, under the appearance of a serpent. He persuaded her to eat (of) the tree that God had forbidden her and promised her that she would become (like) God. When she saw the tree, its fruit pleased her; she ate of it and made 'Adäm eat of it. The garment of light withdrew from them: they knew that they were naked and covered their shameful parts with fig leaves. When God knew their fault, he first cursed the serpent and put

enmity between him and the race of Hëwän. This one also, he cursed, saying: "Give birth in pain and, when you have given birth, may your refuge be with your husband and may he dominate you." To 'Adäm he said: "May the earth be cursed because of you. Eat your bread by the sweat of your face, for you are dust and you will return to dust." He made them garments of skin and clothed them (with them). They went out from the garden of 'Édom at the time of evening after having remained seven seasons in joy and delights. In the morning, at the rising of the sun, 'Adäm burned incense, galbanum, gum tragacanth, and aromatic herbs. 'Adäm remained in the land of 'Éldä cultivating the earth as he had learned (it) from the garden of 'Édom. After that, 'Adäm knew Hëwän and she gave birth for him to Qäyal (= Cain) and, after him, 'Abël (= Abel). When Qäyal and 'Abël had grown up, they brought their offerings before God. When (God) accepted the sacrifice of 'Abël, Qäyal killed 'Abël out of jealousy. 'Adäm remained lamenting the death of his son for four weeks of years. After that was born to him Sët (= Seth) in replacement of 'Abël his son, and through him his (= 'Abël's) memory remained. After him, ('Adäm) begot many sons. While 'Adäm was going out of the Garden, when God saw his tears and his repentance, he gave him the promise of salvation and said to him: "In five thousand five hundred years, I will come into the world, I will be born of your daughter, I will crawl in your place, and I will redeem you by my cross." 'Adäm took from the Garden gold, myrrh, and incense, gave (them) to his son, and wrote for him these words: "During the last days the Son of God will come and he will be born of a virgin. Then the magi will bring him as gifts for him this gold, this myrrh, and this incense." Passing from generation to generation, this writing reached our Lady the holy Virgin Mary. She gave it to our father Pëtros (= Peter), and Pëtros gave it to Qalëmentos (= Clement). When our father 'Adäm knew that his death was near, he called his son Sët and (the latter) approached him in the Cave of Treasures. ('Adäm) said to him: "Bring your sons and the sons of your sons to me before I die." When Sët heard the voice of his father 'Adäm, he left him, tears flowing on his cheeks; he gathered his sons and the sons of his sons and brought them near to him (= 'Adäm). When 'Adäm looked at them, he wept because of their (imminent) separation. When they saw him weeping, all wept in concert, (turned) toward God, and fell on their faces, saying: "How will the earth cover you and (how) will you hide yourself from our eyes?" Then our father 'Adäm blessed them, all his children, and said to Sët: "My son, since this world (is) full of pain and weakness, keep justice and purity by having confidence in God and do not incline toward the voice of Satan and his phantoms that he will show you. At the moment when I die, take my body, bury it

ለሰይጣን ፣፡ ወምትሐታቲሁ ፡ ዘያስተርአየከ ። ወበጊዜ ፣ እመውት ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ወግንዝዎ ፡ በከርቤ ፡ ወሰሊክ ፡ ወሜዓ ፡ ወአንብሩኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐተ ፡ መዛግብት ። ወሀለዎ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ማየ ፡ አይኅ ፡ ወይሠጠሙ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ወኢይተርፍ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፡ ሰማኒቱ ፡ ነፍስ ። ኦወልድየ ፡ ኩን ፡ ቀዋሜ ፡ ለሕዝብከ ፡ ረአዮሙ ፡ ወዕቀቦሙ፣0፣ በፈሪሃ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወምሕሮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ወአማኅፅኖሙ ፣ ከመ ፡ ኢይትአዘዙ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወኢያኅጐሎሙ ፡ ወፍልጦሙ ፡ ለውሉድከ ፤ ወለውሉደ ፡ ውሉድከ ፡ እምውሉደ ፡ ቃየል ፡ ወኢትኅድጎሙ ፡ ይደመሩ ፡ ለግሙራ። ወኢይቅረቡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ወኢኀበ ፡ ተዛውዖቶሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ፣ ወልዱ ፡ ሴት ፡ ወብእሲቱ ፡ ሔዋን ፡ ወአማኅፀኖሙ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ወከርቤ ፡ ወስጎነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያንብርዎሙ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡፣ እስመ ፡ መዋዕለ" ፡ ማየ ፣ አይኅ ፡'መጽአ፣ ይንሥኡ፤ ፡ ሥጋየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወዕጣን ፡ ወከርቤ ፣ ወያንብርዎ ፣ በማእከለ ፤ ምድር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡፤ ይፄውውዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወይነሥኡ ፡ እሎንተ" ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ወከርቤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ምህርካ ፡ ወበህየ ፡ ይትዐቀብ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፣ ይመጽእ ፡ ቃለ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡፣ ወይሠገው ። ወይንሥእዎ ፡ ነገሥት ፤ ወይኤምጎዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወዕጣነ ፡
with myrrh, (cassia), and (stacte) and deposit me in the Cave of Treasures. The water of the flood will come and all creatures will be drowned: only eight souls will remain. My son, be the protector of your people, lead them, and keep them in the fear of God; guide them in the way of salvation and put them under your protection so that they do not obey Satan and (so that he) does not destroy them. Separate your sons and the sons of your sons from the sons of Qäyal and never let them mingle. Let them not approach what (the sons of Qäyal) do nor their entertainments." Then ('Adäm) turned toward his son and his wife Hëwän and entrusted to them the gold, the myrrh, and the incense so that they might place them with him, and he said to them: "The day when the water of the flood comes, let them take my body into the ark with the gold, the incense, and the myrrh and let them deposit it in the middle of the earth. Many days after, (enemies) will remove this city and will take the gold, the incense, and the myrrh with the booty, and they will be kept there (= with the enemies) until the Word of God comes and becomes incarnate. Let the kings take them and they will present them to him, the gold because of his royalty, the incense because he is the God of heaven and earth; as for the myrrh, it is the sign of his sufferings."
በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ አምላከ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ወከርቤኒ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ውእቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ለአዳም ፡ ፪8፻ወ ወዓመተ ፡ በ፻ወወወ፳ዓመት ፡ እምሕይወተ ፡፤ መላልኤል ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በ፪ ፡ ሰዐተ ፡ መዓልት ፡ ዘዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ወበሰዓት" ፡ ዘአፅረፈ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፀአቱ ፣ እምገነት ። አሜሃ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወገነዝዎ፥ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ግንዘተ ፤ ወአምዐዝዎ ፡ በአፈዋት ፤ ብዙኅ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ በዕፀወ ፡ ደብር ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአንበሩ ፣ ሥጋሁ ፡፣ መንገለ ፡ ጽባሒሃ ፡ ለበዐት ፡ ወሜሙ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ዘያበርህ ፡ ወአንበሩ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወርቀ ፡ ከርቤ ፡ ወስቲነ ፡ ወሐተምዋ ፡ ለአንቀጸ ፡ በዐት ፡ በማኅተመ" ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋ ፡ ወሴት ። ወነበሩ ፡ ሴት ፡ ወውሉዱ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይትለአኩ ፡ ለሥጋ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ማየ ፡ አይኅ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታቦት ፡ ወወሰዶ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ አምላክ ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አራራት ። ወእም ሀየ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ ሜም ፡ ወመልከ ፡ ጹዴቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመርሖሙ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወቀበርዎ ፡ መካነ ፡ ቀራንዮ ። ወነበረ ፡ መልከ ፡ ጴዴቅ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ህየ ፡ በዕጣን ፡ ወበፉርባን ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተወልደ ፡ እምወ ለቱ ፡ ተቤዘዎ ፡ በሞቱ ፡ ወሜጦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ርስቱ፤ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አበ ፥፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ አበ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ። እስመ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ወልደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐርሮ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፤ ለፍቅርትሂ ፤ ኮቶሊካዊት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
When 'Adäm was nine hundred and thirty years old, in the one hundred and thirty-fifth year of the life of Maläl'ël (= Mahalaleel), he died at the ninth hour of the day, on Friday, (the day) when he had been created. At the hour when he died had taken place his exit from the Garden. The armies of the angels gathered then with his children; they buried him in a beautiful shroud and perfumed him with many aromatics that were in the trees of the holy mountain. They placed his body to the east of the cave, put before him a lamp that was shining, placed with him the gold, the myrrh, and the incense, and sealed the door of the cave with the seals of 'Adäm, Hëwän, and Sët. Sët and his children remained serving the body of our father 'Adäm until the water of the flood came. Then Noh (= Noah) took the body into the ark and took it with him on the order of God until he had brought it to Mount 'Arärät (= Ararat). From there Sèm (= Shem) and Malka sëdëq (= Melchizedek) took it, an angel of God guiding them, and buried it at the place of the Skull. Malka sëdëq remained performing the service there with incense and sacrifices. When the Son of God came and was born of his (= 'Adäm's) daughter, he redeemed him by his death and replaced him in his first inheritance: he is indeed the father of the prophets and the apostles, the father of the righteous and the martyrs; indeed, it is because of him that the Son of God came into the world. May God, through his prayer, have mercy on his dear 'Atnätëwos (and) also on his dear Kotolikäwit for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ወተወልደ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አበ ፡ ሰሎሞን ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also) was born Noh (= Noah) and was born Däwit (= David) the king, father of Solomon. May their holy blessing and the gift of their help be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]


[p. 514] {#po.46.p514}

### READING FOR THE 7TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ኒለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ዓቢይ፤ ፤ ኢዮአቄም" ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፤ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላዲተ ፡ አምላክ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ተሰምየ ፡ በይአስማት ፡ እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኢዮአቄም ፡ ወዮናኬር ፡ ወሳዶቅ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምዘርአ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወእምነገደ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ፤ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮኣታም ፡ ወልደ ፡ አልኣ ዛር ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኤልዩድ?" ፡ ዘይበጽሕ ፡ በዘመድ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘተካየዶ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘርኡ ፡ ይነግሥ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እስከ ፡ ለዓለም ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ሐና ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ መካን ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ይጹልዩ ፡ ወያስተበሞዑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወትረ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወበመፀልት ፡ ወተወክፈ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ" ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ወአጽገበት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወአእተተት ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ መረረ ፡ ግብርናተ ። ወደለዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሰመይ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ትስብእቱ ፡ ዘመንክር ፤ እምወለቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አስተፍሥሖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በልደታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፣ ማርያም ፡ ወተሐሥየ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወአቅረበ ፣ መሥዋዕቶ ፡ ወአእተተ ፡ ኃፍረቶ ፡ እምላዕሌሁ ፡ ወእምደቂቀ ፡ ዘመዱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አኅደግዋ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ አጥባተ ፡ እማ ፡ ወጾርዋ ፡ ወወሰድዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደሱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዓብፅእዋ፤" ፡ ብፅዓተ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the righteous, the great 'lyo'agëm (= Joachim), father of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God. This saint was called by three names which are 'lyo'agëm, Yonäkër (= Younakhir), and Sädoq (= Sadoc). He was of the lineage of Däwit (= David) and of the tribe of Yehudä (= Judah), for he was the son of 'lyo'ätäm (= Jotham), son of 'Al‘azar (= Eleazar), son of 'Élyud (= Eliud) who traced back through (his) family to Solomon the king, son of Däwit the king to whom God promised that his lineage would reign over the children of Israel forever. Hannä (= Anne), the wife of this righteous man, was sterile. As both prayed and begged God continually, night and day, God accepted their request and gave them a fruit, beautiful (and) sweet (which) satisfied all the men of the universe and took away from them the bitter servitude. ('lyo'agëm) deserved to be called father of Christ because of his (= Christ's) prodigious incarnation from his (= 'lyo'agëm's) daughter. After God had rejoiced him by the birth of our Lady Mary, his heart exulted; he offered his sacrifice and his shame was taken away from him and from (the children of) his family. After they had made Mary leave her mother's breasts, they carried her and led her to the temple of God, according to what (her parents) had vowed her to God. After that, ('lyo'agëm) lived (for) some time and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአጋቦስ ፤ ወቴዮድራ፤፤! ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ ወተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቅሩፋ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ብልየነ!" ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of 'Agäbos (= Agapetus) and Tëyoderä (= Theodora) the martyrs, and the commemoration of the holy abbä Magrufä (= Macrobe), son of abbä Musë (= Moses) of the monastery of Belyana (= Balyana). May God have mercy on us through their prayer to all, and may their blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 8TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮና ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ፻ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ እማንቱ ፡ አጋቢ ፡ ወኦርኒ፤ ፣ ወሶኖነያ፤ ። እሎንቱ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ደናግል ፡ ኮና ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተስሎንቄ ። ወኮና ፡ ያመልካሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ አበዊሆን ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ፈራህያነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአፍቀራ ፡ ድንግልና ፡ ወንጽሕና ፡ ወተሰነአዋ ፡ ተሰናእዎ ፡ መሲ
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, there are the three holy martyrs who are 'Agäbi (= Agape), 'Orni (= Irene), and Sononayä (= Chionia). These holy virgins were people of the city of Taslongë (= Thessalonica). They worshipped our Lord Jesus Christ, for their parents feared God. They loved virginity and purity. They concluded a Christian agreement, devoted themselves to asceticism, and led a great (spiritual) combat that exceeded their youth and their nature. They fasted and prayed continually and kept watch without rest. They went frequently into the monasteries and devoted themselves to asceticism with the virgins,

ሐዊ ፤ ወተጸምዳ ፥ ወተጋደላ ፤ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፣ ዘይፈደፍድ ፣፤ እምውርዙቶን ፡ ወግዕዞን ። ወኮና ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይጸውማ ፡ ወይጹልያ ፡ ወይተግሃ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ፡ ወይትመየጣ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አድባራት ፡ ወይጸመዳ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደናግል ፡ መለኮሳዊያት ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ምክስሚያኖስ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ወአቆመ ፡ አምልኮ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ወከዐወ ፡ ደመ ፡ "ንጹሐ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ወፈርሃ ፡ እላንቱ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ወጐያ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወተኅብኣ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ወነበራ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይጸውማ” ፡ ወይትጋደላ ። ወኮነት ፡ አሐቲ ብእሲት ፡ አረጋዊት ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፤ ትሔውጾን" ፡ ለለ ፡ ሱባዔ ፡ ወትሁቦን ፡ ዘይፈ ቅዳ ፡ ወትሠይጥ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሆን ፡ ወትመጸውት ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ በዘተረፈ!" ፡ እምኔ ሆን ። ወርእያ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እኩይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታበዝኅ ፡ ጸአተ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወተሐውር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወተለዋ ፡ እምርጐቅ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢትሬአዮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ርእያ፤፤ ፡ ቦአት ፣ 'ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ፤" ፡ በዐት፤ ። ወተኀብአ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ትወፅእ ፡ እስመ ፡ መሰሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሀለዋ ፡ ንዋይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዐት ፡ በኅቡእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርሕቀት ፡ይእቲ ፡ አረጋዊት" ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እኩይ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዐት ፡ ወረከቦን ፡ ለእማንቱ ፡ ፅንቄ ፡ ባሕርያት ፡ ክቡራት ፣ መራዕዊሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጴጹልያ ፡ ወይቀውማ ። ወአሰሮን ፡ ወሰሐቦን ፡ እስከ ፡ አብጽሖን ፡ ኀበ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ተስሎንቄ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተስእሎን ፡ በእንተ ፣፤ ሃይማኖቶን ፤ ተአመና ፣ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ እማንቱ፤ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፤ ወያመልካሁ ፡ 'ለዘ ፡ ተሰቅለ፤ ፡ በእንቲአሆን ። ወተምዖን ፡ መኩንን!፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀይለ፤ ፡ ቃሎን፤" ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅሥፍዎን ፡ ወቀሠፍዎን ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወማሕመሜ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢተአዘዛ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወኢቦኣ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሕደቱ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይውግርዎን ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወአሰርዎን ፡ ወገደፍዎን" ፡ ውስተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወመጠዋ ፡ ነፍሳቲሆን ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወነሥኣ ፡ አክሊለ ፡
the nuns. When Maksemiyänos (= Maximian) the impious reigned, when he restored the worship of idols and shed the pure blood of many (people), these virgins became afraid, fled into the mountain, hid themselves in a cave, and remained there fasting and fighting (spiritually). An old Christian woman visited them each week, gave them what they needed, sold the work of their hands, and gave alms to the poor with what they had in excess. A wicked man saw her going out frequently, all the time, from the city and going into the mountain. He followed her from afar in secret—she did not see him—until he saw her enter the cave. He hid himself until she had gone out, for he imagined that she had something hidden in this cave. When the old woman had gone away, this wicked man entered the cave and found these precious stones, betrothed of Christ, in the process of praying standing up. He bound them and dragged them until he had brought them to the governor of the city of Taslongë. When (the latter) questioned them about their religion, they declared before him that they were Christians and worshipped the one who had been crucified for them. The governor became irritated against them because of the strength of their word and ordered them to be scourged; they were subjected to a great and painful scourging. As they did not obey him and did not enter into his impiety, he ordered them to be thrown into the fire; they were bound, they were thrown into the fire, they gave up their souls there, and they received the unfading crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of the heavens. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.
ስምዕ ፡ ዘኢይማስን ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ጸሎቶን ፡ ወበረከቶን ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኮኑ ፤ ፻ወ፪ሰማዕታት ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ሰዐት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለንጉሠ፤፤! ፣ ፋርስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዓገተ ፡ ብሔረ" ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅሩብ ፡ እምብሔሩ ፡ ወፄወወ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ፄዋ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወወሰዶሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኢሰምዕፅዎ ፡ ወኢያመልኩ ፡ ፀሐየ፤ ፡ ወኢከዋክብተ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይም ትሩ፤ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፤ ወመተርዎሙ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አቅሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወሀብተ ፤ ረድኤቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, there were one hundred and fifty martyrs in a single hour by the hand of the king of Färs (= Persia): (the latter) invaded the land of the Christians which (was) near his land, he took many prisoners among them, and took them into his land. As they did not obey him and did not worship the sun and the stars, he ordered their heads to be cut off; they were cut off (with) the sword and they received the crown of martyrdom. May God have mercy on us through their prayer, amen. [On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of Timotëwos (= Timothy) the patriarch. May his prayer and the gift of his help be with his dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 9TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ 8ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ጽሙድ ፡ መስተጋድል ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፡ መለኮስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፍልስጥኤም ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወጻድቃን ። ወሶበ ፡ ወለድዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሐፀንዎ ፡ ፳ዓመተ ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ለአሐዱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕፅኖ ፡ ሕፅነተ ፡ ሠናይተ ፡ መሲሓዊተ ፡ ወይምሕሮ ፡ጥበበ ፡ ወትምህርተ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወተመጠዎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡፤ አረጋዊ ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መንፈሳዌ ፣ ወመሀሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡፣ ጥበበ ፡፤ ወትምህርተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፥፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወል ህቀ ፡ በገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወተወሰከ ፡ ትሩፋተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ይሴብሖ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ወያነብብ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ግብረ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ አው ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ያጸርዕ ፡ ስብሐታተ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ሎቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ደብርቶ ፡ ዛወ ፳ዓመት ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ቀሲሰ፣፤ ወወሰከ ፡ ተጸምዶተ ፡ ወተጋድሎተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በሚመተ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ፲ወ፻ዓመት ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይዌስክ ፡ ተጸምዶተ ፡ ወተጋድሎተ ፤ ወመዋኢ ፡ በኩሉ ፤ ተጋድሎቱ ፡፤ ዘርአ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ መስተቃርን ፡ በውስተ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ እኪተ ፡ ከመ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ተለዓለ ፡ በተጋድሎቱ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምኩሎሙ ፡ መስተጋድላን ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ተረፈ ፡ ግብር ፡ እምገቢረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወተጋድሎ ፡ ዘኢገበርኩ ፡ አነ ። ወኢኅደጎ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምኔሁ ። አላ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ ወአዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ዘቅሩብ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father, devout, (spiritual) combatant, the priest abbä Zosimäs (= Zosimus) the monk. This saint was of the people of the land of Felsetëm (= Palestine), his parents were Christians and righteous. When they had begotten this saint and had raised him for five years, they gave him to an old righteous monk so that he might dispense to him a good Christian education and teach him the wisdom and the law of the Church. The old man received him, made him a spiritual son for himself, and taught him all wisdom and doctrine. After that, he clothed him with the monastic habit and made him a deacon. He grew up doing good and increased in virtue. He praised God continually and read the Scriptures night and day. When he worked with his hands or when he ate his food, he did not cease to praise (God) at any moment. When he had spent forty-five years in the convent, he was ordained a priest and he increased (his) devotion and (his) (spiritual) combat. When he had spent thirteen years in the priesthood—he was increasing (his) devotion and (his) (spiritual) combat and (was) victorious in all his combat—the Enemy, the Adversary, sowed in his heart a bad thought, namely that he (the saint) surpassed in his (spiritual) combat all the (spiritual) combatants who lived in his time. He said to himself: "Does there remain an action among the righteous and ascetic actions that I have not done?" Our Lord Jesus Christ did not reject him far from him but sent him an angel (who) ordered him to go to the monastery which (was) near the river of Yordänos (= the Jordan).

እምፈለገ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ያነ ፡ ጸድቃነ ፡ ፍጹማነ ፡ በገድሎሙ ። ወአእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሕፁፅ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ወይጌይስ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወኀደረ ፡ በኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወተጋደለ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ልማዶሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይበጽሕ ፡ጾም ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ጓመዓልት፣ ቅድስት ፤ ይጸውሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፡ ፳8ሱባዔ ፡ ቀዳማየ ፡ እምጾም ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ወበዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ ይነሥኡ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ። ወበሳኒታ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፡ እምደ ብር ፤ ወእሙንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፣፤ ይዜምሩ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዘ፳ወዷእግዚአብሔር ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ሊተ?” ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጸሜቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይትፌጸም ፡ ፀአቶሙ ፡ እምደብር ፡ ይጹልዩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፣ ደብር ፡ ወይትአምሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ወይባርክ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ አበ ፣ ምኔት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይዘረዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወይትጋደሉ ፡ ለለ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ከመ፣ ኢይትረአዩ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ቢጾሙ ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ፳8 እምኔሆሙ ፡ ቢጾ ፡ ዘቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ይጐይይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይርአይ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ጽንፍ ። ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ዞሲማስ ፡፤ ለለዓመት ፡ ይወፅእ ፤ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወይዓዔይል፤፤ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወይስእሎ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡፤ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይትናዘዝ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወእንዘ"፤" ፡
He got up, went, and arrived at this monastery. He found there righteous old men, accomplished in their (spiritual) combat. He knew that he was inferior to them and (that) there were in the world (people) who were more accomplished and better than he. He remained with them in this monastery and fought (spiritually) with them for many years. It was the custom of the monks of this convent, when the great fast arrived, the forty holy days, to fast in the monastery for one week, the first of the holy fast, and, on Sunday, to receive the holy Mysteries. The next day, they went out of the monastery singing Psalm 27—God illuminates me—until the end. When their exit from the monastery was finished, they prayed at the door of the monastery, said their farewells to each other, and the superior of the monastery blessed them. Then they dispersed into the desert and each of them fought (spiritually) apart, so that the face of his companions might not be seen (by him). If one of them saw his companion who (was) near him, he fled to another side so as not to see the face of the other. The saint Zosimäs went out each year with them, wandered in the desert, and asked God to show him someone by whom he would be comforted.


[p. 520] {#po.46.p520}
ይዔይል፤ ፡ ረከባ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ ግብጸዊት ፡ ገዳማዊት ። ወተጠየቀ ፡ እምኔሃ" ፡ እፎ፤ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምጽአታ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወነገረቶ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ገድላ ፡ እምጥንታ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜታ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኃሠሠት ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ከመ፣ ያምጽእ ፡ ላቲ ፡ "ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ላቲ! ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወመጠዋ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሐወጻ ፡ በዳግም ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወረከባ ፡ በዘ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ወቀበራ፤ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤ ፡ ደለዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ብፁዓዊያን ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ወጽድቆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፲ወ፳ቿዓመት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ነበረ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 8ዓመተ፣፤ ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብር ፤ 3ወ፻ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ፤ ፡ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋ
While he was wandering, he found Märyäm (= Mary) the Egyptian, the hermit. He inquired of her about the way in which her coming into the desert had taken place. She told him everything that had happened to her and all her life from the beginning to the end. Then she asked him to bring her the holy Mysteries. He brought (them) to her a year later and administered (them) to her. After that, he visited her the following year, found her dead, and buried her. This saint deserved to go to the land of the blessed, and it is he who wrote their life and revealed their actions and their justice. When he had completed ninety-eight years, he died in peace. Of this (time) he spent five years in the world before entering the monastery and ninety-three years devoting himself to devotion and asceticism for God. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ደል ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፤ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ጸድቃን ፡ ዘብሔረ ፡ ብፁዓን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ፡ ማኅበ ራነ ፡ ኢስድሮስ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ዘ፲ ወርኅ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ። በረከቶሙ ፡ ወሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አስተርአየት ፤ ትእምርት ፡ ዓባይ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅ ዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰኑትዩ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዓርገ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹም ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ጾመ ፡ በውስተ ፣ ደብረ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መነኮሳት፤! ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰቡዓ ፡ ሆሳዕና ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይማህርኩ ፡ አድባራተ ፤" ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ምሥራቀ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርስ ፡ ወአስይፍቲሆሙ ፡ ምሉሓት ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርቅ ዎሙ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምዓረብ ፡ ተንበላት ፣ ወኅሠሠ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፃኡ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ፋሲካ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይትፈለጥ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ እፈጽም ፡ በዓለ ፡ ፋሲካ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሐሙስ ፡ ዘኪዳን ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘፍሥሕ ፤ ዘቦቱ ፡ ሐፀበ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ወተወሰከ ፡ ክላሆሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ዓረብ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰኑትዩ፤፤ ፡ ድንጋፄሆሙ ፡ ለሕ ዝቡ ፡ ነሥአ፡ በትረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ዘዲቤሁ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡'ወአኀዘ ፡ ይፍቅድ፣ ከመ ፡ ይፃእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይቴይሰኒ ፤ መዊት" ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆጳሳት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ወከልእዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይፃእ፣፡ ወአጽንዓ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ወናዘዞሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዓረብ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ወበትረ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ድኅሬሆሙ ፡ ወጐዩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰራዊት ፡ ብዙኃን ፡'እንዘ ፡ ይሰድድዎሙ ቶ ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ ኢተመይጡ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ኅበ ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፤ ወኢገብሩ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡፤
[On this day, there are also the righteous of the land of the blessed, the martyrs companions of 'Isderos (= Isidore) (and) a small child of ten months with his father and his mother. May their blessing and the gift of their help be with their dear 'Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.] On this day also a great miracle was manifested by the hand of our father the holy abbä Sanutyu (= Shenoute), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. This (happened) thus: he had gone up into the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis) to perform the great fast in the monastery of the holy abbä Magäres (= Macarius) with the monks. When it was the holy week, Muslim Arabs gathered and came to the desert of 'Asqêtes to plunder the monasteries. They stood on a rock to the east of the church of the holy abbä Magäres, their swords drawn in their hands to strip the people. The bishops and the monks gathered near him (= the patriarch), telling him what the situation was with the Muslim Arabs, and they asked to leave the desert before the feast of Easter. The saint said to them: "As for me, I will not leave the desert until I have finished the feast of Easter." When it was the fifth day of the new covenant of Easter during which our Lord Christ washed the feet of his disciples, the clamors of the Muslim Arabs increased. When abbä Sanutyu saw the terror of the people, he took his staff on which (was) the sign of the cross, wanting to go out toward them (= the Arabs), saying: "It is better for me to die with the people of God." The bishops and the monks seized him and prevented him from going out. He strengthened their heart and comforted them. Then he went out toward the Muslim Arabs, his staff in his hand. When the Muslims saw him, they turned their heels and fled as if many soldiers were chasing them. From that day, these Muslims did not return toward the holy places and did not do them (any more) harm. May God have


[p. 522] {#po.46.p522}
እኩየ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አባ ፡ ሰንትዩ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
mercy on us through the prayer of this father abbä Sanutyu and may his blessing be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 10TH OF MIYAZYA

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father, the (spiritual) combatant abbä Yeshaq (= Isaac), the disciple of the eminent father, abbä 'Ablo (= Apollon). This saint despised this world and all its delights and sweetnesses. He went up into the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis), became a monk, became the disciple of the holy father abbä 'Ablo, and remained serving him for twenty-five years. He led a great (spiritual) combat so well that his body withered and (he) killed all the thoughts of his body. He strengthened his integrity and gained tranquility at the moment of prayers and offices. He stood there in fear, his hands joined behind him and his head lowered, and wept until the office or the prayer was finished. When he went out of the office, he never joined anyone that day and did not leave the door of his cell open so that no one would enter his place. When (the monks) questioned him and said to him: "Why do you not converse with the one who wants to converse with you at the moment of prayer and at the moment of the office?", he answered and said to them: "Each thing (has) a time that suits it." When the moment of his death approached, a group of monks gathered near him to be blessed by him and to recommend themselves to his prayer. They questioned him and said to him: "Why did you flee from men?" He answered and said: "I did not flee from men but from Satan, for if a man holds a lit lamp and finds himself in a draft, does (the lamp) not go out? Likewise (for us), if our heart shines at the moment of prayer and at the moment of the office, when we join men and converse together, our heart darkens." When this saint had finished his spiritual life and had pleased God by his combat, God wanted to make him rest from the pains of this transitory world: he was slightly ill and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.
አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብርኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት፤፤ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ዘይሰመይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ተሮይክ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአ በው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ጳጳሳት ፡ ድ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምውሉድዶ ፡ ልሂቃነ ፡ሀገረ ፡ ምስር ፡ ወብዑላኒሃ፤" ። ወኮነ ፡ ጸሓፌ ፡ ወማእምረ ፡ ወጠቢበ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ መጸሕፍተ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä Gabr'ël (= Gabriel), patriarch of the city of Alexandria, who is called "Son of Taroyk" (= Ibn Toraïk); among the patriarch fathers, he is the seventieth. This saint was of the children of the great (personages) of the city of Mesr (= Old Cairo) and
