ብዙኃተ ፡ ቅብጢ ፡ ወዓረቢ፤ ። ወተርጐመ፤ ፣፡ ብዙኃነ ፣ እምኔሆሙ ፡ 'ወአጽንዓ ፡ ብዙኃነ ፡ እምኔሆሙ! ። ወኃረይዎ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅሳት ፡ ወሥዩማን ፡ ወሊቃውንተ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ። ወሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ፳8አረጋዊ ፤ ሶርያዊ ፣ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አባ ፡፣ ዮሴፍ ። ወኮነ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡እምኀበ ፡እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይሬኢ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይትናገር ፡ በዘይከውን ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ይኩን ። ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ አረጋውያን ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ ከመ፣ ያይድፆሙ ፡ ወይክሥት ፡ ሎሙ?" ፡ መኑ ፡ ዘይቴይስ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ። ወአውሥኦሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ዘይሰመይ!" ፡ ወልደ ፡ ተሮይክ ፡ ወነገርዎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ለኤኢስ ፡ ቆልሳት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ። ወሠምሩ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ወተሰነአዉ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ፤ ። ወሶበ ፡ 'ዓርገ ፡ ኀበ" ፡ ገዳመ ፣ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ተአሚኖ፤ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በተፍጻሜተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ባስልዮስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ወኮነ ፡ 8ምስለ ፡ መለኮቱ ። ወይቤልዎ ፡ መነኮሳተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡፣ ልማድነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ፈርሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየሐሊ ፡ ዘኢኮነ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ ከመ ፡ መለኮቱ ፡ ቱስሕት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ትስብእቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሐቲት ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ተሠርዓት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቃል ፣፤ ወዘይተልዋ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍልጠት ፡ ወኢቱስሕት ፡ ወኢትድምርት"። ። ወተወክፍዋ፤ ፡ ወተሠርዓ ፡ እስከ ፡ዛቲ ፡
(of) its rich. He was a scribe, learned and wise. He copied with his hand many Coptic and Arabic books, commented on many of them, and learned many of them by heart. The bishops, the leaders, and the elders of the people chose him to name him patriarch. There was in the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis) a spiritual Syrian old man by the name of abbä Yosëf (= Joseph). A grace (coming) from God was upon him, he saw by the Holy Spirit and spoke of what was occurring before (it) occurred. The old monks gathered near him and asked him to make known to them and to reveal who was the best for this distinguished office. He answered them and said to them: "A man who is called Son of Taroyk." The monks repeated this proposal to the bishops. All accepted it and agreed on him; they seized him and consecrated him patriarch. When he went up to the desert of 'Asqêtes, he added to the prayer of the Creed, which is at the end of the liturgy of Bäselyos (= Basil), the words "and he became One with the divinity." The monks of the desert of 'Asqêtes said to him: "For us, this is not our custom," for they feared that the one who was not learned would think that the divinity (was) mixed with the humanity. After a great inquiry, this formula was instituted and what follows it says: "without separation nor mixture nor confusion." (The monks) accepted it and it was instituted until this day.
ዕለት ። ወሠርዓ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ሠናያተ ፤ ወአውገዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይቅብርዎሙ ፡ ለምውታን ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፡ ዘምስር ፡ ወለቃህራ ። ወአውገዞሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡፤ በኀቤሆሙ ፡ ዕቁባት ፤ ወያውፅእዎሙ ፡ እምአብያቲሆሙ ፡ ወይስድድዎም ። ወደረሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ወኩነኔ ፡ ፍትሕ ፤ በእንተ ፡ ርስቶሙ ፡ ለምውታን ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ካልኣን ፡ ምግባራት ፣ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ሥሩ ዓን ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡፤ እስከ ፡ ዮም ። ወተርጐመ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፤ ብዙኃተ ፣ እመጻሕፍተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወመ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፤ ሚመቱ ፡ ፪ወ፻ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦ ሳተ ፡ ወካህናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወኢነሥአ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ፳ድርጊመ ። ወሶበ ፡ አመንደቦ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፤ ወኀሠሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ በግፍዕ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ላዕ ሌሁ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ዓይን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቅብዎ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ መኳንንት ፣ ወሥዩማን ፡ እምሕዝቡ፤ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፣ ኢነሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እም 8፳እምሕዝብ ፡ ወኢእም ንዋየ ፡ አብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ ወኢምድረ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ አስተጋብኡ ፡ እምአብያቲሆሙ ፡ ፲፻ዲናረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ" ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ጽድቆ ፡ ወትሩፋቲሁ ፡ ለሰብእ ። ወሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በራእይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፣ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጸውሩ ፡ በእደዊሆሙ ፡ መስቀላተ ፡ ወማዕጠንታተ ፡ ወወንጌላተ ። ወተአምኅዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነሐውጽከ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ንትመየጥ ፡ ኅቤከ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ወንነሥአከ ፡ ምስሌነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነቅሀ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆአሳት ፡ ወለካህናት ፡ እለ ፡
In his time, this father instituted many (and) good ways of acting; he forbade burying the dead in the churches of Mesr and of Qäherä (= Cairo). He forbade all those who (had) concubines at their homes and (ordered?) them to be made to leave their houses and to be driven out. This father drafted a code and rules of law concerning the inheritance of the dead and concerning other matters. They (are) established in the Church until today. He commented on many books among the books of the Church. During the time of his office, he consecrated fifty-three bishops and (ordained) many priests. He did not receive from them a single dirham. When the king of Egypt at that time persecuted him and demanded money from him by violence, (this king) imposed spies on him to observe him. As the magistrates and the leaders of his people knew that he had received nothing from anyone of the people, neither from the goods of the churches, nor (from) the lands of the poor, because of this, they gathered from their houses a thousand gold pieces and gave them to the king for him. When his death was near, God wanted to reveal his justice and his virtues to men. He was slightly ill and saw in a vision as if many priests and monks coming near him. They carried in their hands crosses, censers, and gospels. They greeted him and said to him: "We, we have come near you to visit you. We will return then near you after the end of the year and we will take you with us." When he awoke, he told the bishops and the priests who were near him what he had seen, then he


[p. 526] {#po.46.p526}
ሀለዉ ፡ በኅቤሁ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ደዌ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ዓመት ፡ ሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወርእዮሙ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ አለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወተአምኅዎ ፡ ወተፈሥሐ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፲ ወ 8ዓመተ ።
recovered from this illness. After the end of the year, he was slightly ill and saw again the priests and the monks who had come near him before; they greeted him and he rejoiced with them. He then returned his soul into the hand of God and died in peace. All the days of his office on the seat of Märgos (= Mark) the evangelist were fourteen years.

In the time of this father the king of Ethiopia gave an order to abbä Mikä'ël (= Michael), the metropolitan (of Ethiopia), and said to him: "Consecrate bishops for the land of Ethiopia." The metropolitan said to the king: "I cannot consecrate other bishops, if it is not on the order of the patriarch." The king of Ethiopia sent a letter to this father abbä Gabr'ël soliciting him on this subject. Likewise, he sent a letter to the king of Egypt asking him to give an order to this father (on this subject so that) this father would give a mandate to abbä Mikä'ël, the metropolitan of Ethiopia, to consecrate for him other bishops in addition to (those that he himself, Gabr'ël) had consecrated previously. The king of Mesr (= Egypt) ordered this father abbä Gabr'ël to give a mandate to abbä Mikä'ël, the metropolitan of Ethiopia, to do for the king of Ethiopia what (the latter) wanted. The great ones entered near the king of Mesr and said to him: "If the bishops multiply in the land of Ethiopia, they will take boldness and will consecrate a metropolitan of their (own) movement and they will never return toward you." When the king of Mesr heard that, he gave an order to this father patriarch abbä Gabr'ël and said to him: "Send a letter and forbid the king of Ethiopia so that he does not name henceforth bishops for the land of Ethiopia." The patriarch sent a letter, as the king of Mesr had ordered (it) to him. When this letter of prohibition reached the king of Ethiopia, the palace of the latter was destroyed by fire. There was in his land famine and plague, the rain was prevented from falling on them (= the inhabitants), and great misfortunes happened to them, for the king had transgressed the commandment of God. After that, the king of Ethiopia turned toward God, repented, and sent a letter to the king of Mesr asking him to order this father (= Gabr'ël) to dispatch and to release him from the prohibition. This father wrote a letter of blessing and sent it to the king and to all of Ethiopia, blessing them. When this blessing reached the land of Ethiopia, God removed his anger from them (= the inhabitants) and sent his clemency and his mercy. The rain fell on them and the famine and the plague withdrew from them. They rejoiced with a great joy. This is what happened in the time of this king. May the prayer and the blessing of this father be with us, amen.

### READING FOR THE 11TH OF MIYAZYA

፡ ባቲ ፡ ወይጹውዓ ፤ ወትመጽእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ኀቤሃ ። ወኮነት ፡ ትከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕሊናሃ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያሌብዋ ፡ ምክንያቶ ፡ ለጸላኢ ፡ ወመሣግሪሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰደድዎ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ አትናስዮስ፤ ፡ እመንበሩ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፣ እስክንድርያ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ላቲ ፡ ድርሳናተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወፈነዎሙ ፡ ኅቤሃ ። ወነበረት ፡ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ እስከ ፡ ልህቀት ፣ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ መንፈ ሳዊ ። ወርእየት ፡ እምነ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፳እለ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ወአትናስዮስ፤ ፡ ወጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጢሞቴዎስ ፡ ወቴዎፍሎስ ። ወደረሰት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትምህርታተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ ወበቀዔተ ፡ ቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ደረሰት ፡ በጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘውስቴታ ። ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተመሀረቶ ፡ እምእሉ ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ። ወሰአ ልዋ ፡ ካልኣን ፣ ወይቤልዋ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተናገረ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ቦኑ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቤልዎ ፡ አርምም ፡ አው ፡ ይገሥጽዎ ፡ አው ፡ ይፌጽሙ ፡ እዘኒሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይስምፅዎ ። ወአውሥአቶ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይቤላ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ኢይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ይበሉ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየዓድሞ ፡ ሰሚዓ፤፤ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ግሙራ። በከመ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አንበረ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በቅድሜከ ፡ ማዕደ ፡ ወዲቤሃ ፡ መባልዕት ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ሠናያት ፤ ወሕሠማት ፡፤ ኢይትከሀለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትበሎ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ወአርሕቆ ፡ እምቅድሜየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ የሐስመኒ ፡ አላ ፡ ተኀድጎ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወትበልዕ ፡ እምካልኡ ። ወትቤ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አልቦ ፣ ዘይመውኦ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፣ ወያኃሥሮ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy and pure abbess Tëwodorä (= Theodora). This saint was of the children of the great personages of the city of Alexandria and of its rich, they were Christians. This saint desired to clothe herself with the monastic habit and fought (spiritually) for the name of Christ. Her parents had had (made) for her sumptuous garments and ornaments of gold and silver of great price, for they were thinking of marrying her; they had no children outside of her. As she did not want the marriage of this world, she took all the effects that her parents had had (made) for her, handed them to someone who sold them, and gave (the proceeds) to the poor and the needy. She built a church outside the city of Alexandria on the side of the west. After that, she went near the holy father abbä 'Atnäsyos (= Athanasius), patriarch of the city of Alexandria. He shaved her hair and made her a nun in a monastery among the monasteries of virgins which (are) outside the city of Alexandria. She devoted herself there to increased asceticism, led a spiritual combat, and deserved to see divine visions. She saw the angels and the demons; she prayed and discerned their actions. It was given to her to know the thoughts and she examined them. The holy abbä 'Atnäsyos rejoiced at her (subject), he called her and she came, and he himself also went to her. She discovered to him all her thoughts and the saint made her penetrate the ruses of the Enemy and his snares. When they drove the holy abbä 'Atnäsyos from his seat of the city of Alexandria, he wrote to her many edifying (letters) and sent them to her. This saint lived until an advanced old age, she was firm in the spiritual combat. She saw five of the patriarchs, who are 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander), 'Atnäsyos, Pëtros (= Peter), Timotëwos (= Timothy), and Tëwoflos (= Theophilus). This saint composed many and useful teachings. She composed some of them by the grace of the Holy Spirit which (was) in her and the others, according to what she had learned from the patriarchs. People questioned her and said to her: "If a man holds bad remarks, is it necessary to say to him: 'Be silent' or to rebuke him or to plug one's ears so as not to hear him?" She answered the one who said that and said to him: "It is not necessary to say anything of that, but it is necessary that hearing his words not be pleasant to him (= the listener) at all; just as if a man places before you a table and, on it, many good and repulsive dishes, it is not possible for you to say to him: 'Take that and move it from before me, for that is repulsive to me,' but you leave it and you eat of other (things)." She also said: "No one can defeat Satan and confound him except by fasting, by prayer, and by humility." All the days of her life

[p. 530] {#po.46.p530}
"በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበትሕትና ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወታ ፡ ፻ዓመት ፣ ወአዕረፈት ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆቆስ ፡ ዘጋዛ ። ጸሎቱ ፤ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡፤ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለአባ ፣ በኪሞስ ፡ ዘእለ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወስምዖን ፡ ዘለንጽ ፡ ወታውድራ ፡ መኩንን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
were one hundred years old, and she died in peace. May her prayer and blessing be with us, amen. On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of the holy Yohannes (= John), bishop of Gäzä (= Gaza). May his prayer be with us, amen. [On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of abbä Bakimos (= Pachomius?) of Alexandria, (of) Sem'on (= Simon), of Lanes, and of Täwderä (= Theodora) the governor (7). May God have mercy through their prayer upon His dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 12TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡፤ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡እልስክንድሮስ፤ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ተሠይመ ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስግድ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወይትባ ረክ ፡ እምነ ፡ መካናት ፡ ቅዱሳት ፤ ወይትመየጥ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ኤጴስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ በርኪሶስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ልህቀ፤ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፻ወ፲ዓመት ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድግ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስና ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡፤ ወኢኀደግዎ ፡ ሕዝቡ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ አስምዖሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ለሰብአ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ እንዘ ፣፤ ይብሎሙ ፡ ፃኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ 'አንቀጸ ፣ ሀገር ፡ ወዘይበውእ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ አኃዝዎ ፡ ወሚምዎ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ። ወወፅኡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፣ ወአኃዝዎ ፡ ወዓበየ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ ወኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ እኅድግ ፡ መርዔትየ ፡ ዘመኒ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወእርአይ" ፡ ካልኣነ ። ወአይድፅዎ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘሰምዕዎ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጠየቀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በፈቃደ!" ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፤ ወአምከርዎ ፡ አበው ፡-እለ ፡ጉቡኣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓል፤፤ ፡ ዝክቱ ፡ ወተወክፈ ፡ ቃሎሙ ፡ ወጸሐፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ወፈነዋ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሎሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ስረዩ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወኢትሕዝኑ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ወሚሙ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ኤስ ፡ ቆፅሰ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ዚአየ ፡ ወአንትሙ ፡ ፍቱሓን ። ወፈነወ ፡ ይእተ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምሀገረ ፡ ቀጳዶቅያ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ፤። ፡ በበዓል ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the glorious angel Mikä'ël (= Michael) the archangel. May his intercession be with us, amen. On this day also died the holy father 'Eleskenderos (= Alexander), bishop of the city of Jerusalem. This saint had been consecrated bishop of the land of Qapadoqyä (= Cappadocia) and had come to the city of Jerusalem to prostrate himself, to be blessed by the Holy Places, and to return to his country. There was in Jerusalem a righteous bishop by the name of Barkisos (= Praxias?); he was advanced in age and had reached one hundred and ten years; he had wished many times to abandon the episcopate, but his people had not let him (do so). When this saint 'Eleskenderos had finished his business and wished to return to his country, Qapadoqyä, God caused the people of Jerusalem to hear a voice (coming) from heaven saying to them: "Go out to the gate of the city and, the first who enters, seize him and establish him as bishop over you." They went out to the gate of the city, found this saint abbä 'Eleskenderos, and seized him. He refused and said to them: "I (am) bishop of the land of Qapadoqyä and I cannot abandon my flock over which Christ has established me and keep another." (The people) informed him of the voice they had heard from heaven and what it had said to them concerning him. When he was assured that this (was taking place) by the will of God and (that) the fathers who (were) gathered for this feast advised (it) to him, he accepted their word and wrote a letter—he sent it (to the people of) his country—telling them what had happened to him. He said to them: "Forgive me, do not grieve over me, and appoint a bishop over you in my place: you are free." He sent this letter with the people of the land of Qapadoqyä who were at the feast.


[p. 532] {#po.46.p532}
ወእምዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ"፤ ፡ ምስለ ፡ በርኪሶስ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ መጠነ ፡፤ ፳ዓመት ፡ ወዘይፈደፍድ ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ በርኪሶስ! ። ወተመጠወ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ፤፤ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፤ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወዓቀቦሙ ፡ ለመርዔቱ ፡ ዓቂበ ፡ መንፈሳዌ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሐዋርያት ። ወአኀዞ ፡ መክሲሞስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ከሓዲ ፡ ወኩነኖ ፡ ኩኔኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሞቅሖ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይሔሊ'! ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወአጥፍኦ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለውእቱ ፣፤ መክሲሞስ ፡ አላዊ ፤ ፍጡነ ፡ ወአውፅእዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነግሠ ፡ ገርድያኖስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ህድአት ፡ ወተሰሰለ፤" ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሕዳጠ! ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሞተ ። ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ፊልዕስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መሃይምነ ፡ ወየአምን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወፈትሖሙ ፡ ለተአማንያን ፡ እለ ፡ ሙቁሓን ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወአክበሮሙ ፡ ክብረ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በህድአት ፡ ወበሰላም ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ዳኪዮስ!፤" ፡ 'ላዕለ ፡ ፊልኦስ" ፡ ወቀተሎ፤፤ ፡ ወነግሠ ፡ ህየንቴሁ ፡ ወአመንደቦሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወአኀዞ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ወለብዙኃን ፡ እምሊቃውንተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኩነኖሙ ፡ በዘዘ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሠፎ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፤ ማሕመመ ፡ በድቡሳት ፡ በሊሓት ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ተሰብሩ ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሠ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስሐብዎ ፡ በእግሩ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፤ ወይግድፍዎ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘ ። ወነበረ ፡ ግዱፈ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአመን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስከ ፡ መጠወ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡
Then this father 'Eleskenderos remained with Barkisos the elder for five years and more. After that, the father Barkisos died; 'Eleskenderos received his flock of the city of Jerusalem and he guarded his flock with a spiritual guard like the apostles. Maksimos (= Maximin), the impious prince, seized him and subjected him to great torture with varied tortures. Then he imprisoned him until he had reflected on what he would do with him. God quickly annihilated this Maksimos the infidel and this father was brought out of prison. When Gardeyänos (= Gordian) reigned, tranquility returned and the persecution against the Christians receded somewhat, then he died. Filpos (= Philip) reigned in his place; he was a believer and believed in our Lord Christ. He freed the confessors who were imprisoned because of the name of Christ and bestowed great honors upon them. This father remained in tranquility and peace until Däkiyos (= Decius) rose against Filpos and killed him; he reigned in his place and persecuted the Christians severely. He seized this father and many of the leaders of the Church and subjected them to varied tortures, particularly this father. He subjected him to a great (and) painful scourging with sharp masses until his ribs were broken in his chest. Then he ordered him to be dragged by the feet to the prison and to be thrown into it, and it was done to him as (the emperor) had ordered. He remained lying in the prison confessing our Lord Christ until he delivered his soul into the hand of God. He inherited the eternal kingdom that (God) has prepared for His saints. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ውስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወወረሰ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ዘለዓለም" ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ለቅዱሳኒሁ፤ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወአድኃኖ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ ፀባብ ፣ ዘአምዓም ፡ ዘአሞቅሖ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ሴዴቅያስ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአውፅኦ ፡ አቤሜሌክ ፡ ኢትዮጵያዊ ፡ገብሩ ፡ ለሊቀ፡፡ ፡ ሐራሁ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ባረኮ ፤ ኤርምያስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይርአይ ፤ ብድ ወታ ፡ ለኢየሩሳሌም ፣፤ ወኢይጥዐም ፡ መረረ ፡ ፄዋዌ ፡ ወመንሱተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ወኖመ ፡፳ወኒ ዓመተ ። ወምስሌሁ ፡ ወይን ፡ ወበለስ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ኢማሰነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምፄዋዌ ። ወአውፅአ ፡ እምወይን ፡ ወእምበለስ ፡ ወኩኩሕ ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ለኤርምያስ ፡ ነቢይ ። በእንተዝ ፣ አዘዙነ ፣ መምህራነ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ንግበር ፡ በዐሎ ፡ ለመልአክ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ለለ ፡ ወር ። ትንብልናሁ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also), God sent Mikä'ël (= Michael) the archangel to 'Érmeyäs (= Jeremiah) the prophet and delivered the latter from the narrow mud prison where King Sédëgeyäs (= Zedekiah) had imprisoned him; 'Abëmelek (= Abimelech) the Ethiopian, the servant of the king's general, brought him out. Immediately 'Érmeyäs blessed him and told him that he would not see the devastation of Jerusalem nor taste the bitter and calamitous captivity. It happened thus for him; he slept for sixty-seven years. (He had) with him grapes and figs; they did not rot until the children of Israel returned from captivity. (Mikä'ël) brought him (= 'Abemëlek) out of the vineyard, the fig trees, and (the) cave and gave him to 'Érmeyäs the prophet. Because of this, the doctors of the Church have prescribed for us to celebrate the feast of the glorious angel, the archangel Mikä'ël, on the twelfth of each month. May his intercession be with His dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ጣመው ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of the saint 'Entonyos (= Anthony), bishop of the city of Tämaw (= Tamouiah or Thmoui). May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 534] {#po.46.p534}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፤ አፅረፉ ፡ ጋስዮስ ፡ ወኤስድሮስ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ፣ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also) died Gäsyos and 'Ésderos (= Isidore) to whom appeared Yohannes (= John) the Baptist. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 13TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲፻ጀለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ፪መነኮሳት ፡ ቅዱሳን ፣ ጽሙዳን ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ አባ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ አርድእቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሚልዮስ፤ ፡ ዘኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በደብረ ፡ ኩራሳን" ። ወናሁ ፡ ጸሐፍነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ስምዖሙ ፡ ወተጋድሎቶሙ ፡ አመ ፣ ፳ወቿለዝንቱ ፡ ወርኅ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አቡሆሙ ፣ መንፈሳዊ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ባቲ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካራ ፡ ለዲዮናሳ ፡ ላእክት ፡ ዘእምሐዋርያዊያት ፡ እለ ፡ ጫምዎን ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ጸሎታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, there are the two holy and devout monks, the martyrs abbä 'lyäsu (= Joshua) and abbä Yosëf (= Joseph), disciples of the holy father Milyos (= Milius), who were martyrs in the mountain of Kuräsän (= Khorassan). Behold, we have written all their martyrdom and their (spiritual) combat on the 28th of this month; that is the day when their spiritual father became a martyr. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen. On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of Diyonäsä (= Denise), the deaconess who (is) among the apostolic (deaconesses) whom the disciples of our Lord Christ instituted. May her prayer be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ መድልዮስ” ፡ ሰማዕት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አኮሊሳቴሞስ ፡ ወ 8፪መነኮሳት ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of the saint Madleyos (= Midius?) the martyr. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen and amen. [On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of our father 'Akolisätémos and (of) four monks. May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnatéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 14TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲8ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ፡ መክሲሞስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፣ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምፕልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲ወ፳ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይፈርሆ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምግባራቲሁ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ ርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወተምህረ ፡ ጽሕፈተ ፡ መጻሕፍተ፤ ፡ ዮናናዊያን" ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ጥበቦሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ፍጹመ ፣ ወለባዌ ፡ ወማእምረ ፡ ፈድፋዶ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ዮናናዊያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተምህረ ፡ ትምህርተ ፡ መጻሕፍተ" ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወሕገ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሜሞ ፡ አብ ፡ ያሮክላ፣ ዲያቆነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወኮነ ፡ ጽሙደ ፡ ወመስተጋድለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሚመት ፣ ዘተሠይመ ፡ ባቲ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ ኃረይዎ ፡ አበው ፡ ኤሏስ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy, spiritual father, Maksimos (= Maximus), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; among the patriarch fathers, he is the fifteenth. This father was a man who feared God in all his actions. He was from the people of the city of Alexandria. His parents were Christians of right faith. He learned the writing of the books of the Greeks and all their wisdom. He was accomplished, knowledgeable, and very learned in the language of the Greeks. Then he learned the teaching of the books of the Church and the law of God. The father Yäroklä (= Heraclas) ordained him deacon of the Church of the city of Alexandria. Then the father Diyonäsyos (= Dionysius) ordained him priest. He was devout and ascetic in all the offices to which he had been appointed. When the father Diyonäsyos died, the learned bishop fathers chose this (saint) to appoint him

ቆኦሳት ፡ ማእምራን ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ወተሠይመ ፡ በፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወኮነ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጸም ፡ ማኅበር ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ አንጾኪያ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፤ ሰሜፃይጢ' ። ወሶበ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ሚመቱ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልእክታት ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ሥዩማነ" ፡ ማኅበር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኮነ ፣ በውስተ ፡ ማኅበር ፡፣ ወዘከመ ፡ አውገዝዎ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሰሜፃይጢ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡፤ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ በግዕዙ ። ወአንበበ ፡ መልእክቶሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ጸሐፈ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡፤ ወፈነዋ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መልእክታት ፡ ዘማኅበር ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሐውርተ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኅበ ፡ ብሔረ ፡፤ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ ወብሔረ ፡ ኖባ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይኤዝዞሙ፤ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸልዩ፤፤ ፡ወያስተብሦዑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእትት ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ግዕዞ ፡ ወስሕተቶ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ ሰሜፃይጢ'"" ፣ ከሐዲ ። ወተወክፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ወኢነበረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከሐዲ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፣ ሰሜፃይጢ!" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሕዳጠ! መዋዕለ ፡ ወአጥፍኦ ፡፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወአእተተ ፡ ሃይማኖቶ ፡ እኪተ ፡
patriarch on the seat of Märgos (= Mark) the evangelist, and he was consecrated by the will of God. The father Diyonäsyos had died before the council that had gathered in the city of 'Ansokiyä (= Antioch) concerning Päwlos (= Paul) who (was) from the city of Samëdaäyti (= Samosata) was finished. When this father sat on his patriarchal seat, letters reached him from the leaders of the council concerning what had happened in the council and on how they had excommunicated Päwlos the impious Samosatene and all those who believed in his opinion. (The patriarch) read their letters before the priests of the city of Alexandria. Then he also wrote a letter of his own and sent it with the letters of the council to all the provinces of Egypt, to the land of Ethiopia, and the land of Nobä (= Nubia); he prescribed to them (= to the inhabitants of these regions) to pray and to beseech God so that He might extirpate from the world the opinion and the error of Päwlos the impious Samosatene. God accepted their request and the impious Päwlos the Samosatene lived only a few (more) days; God destroyed him quickly and extirpated his evil faith from the world.
እምውስተ ፡ ዓለም ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ምስራቅ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ማኒ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ስሕተ፤ ፡ ወሰመየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ጳራቅሊጦስ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሶርያ ፡ ወተዋሥአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ፳ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆይስ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አርኪላዎስ፤ ፡ ወሞኦ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ስሕተቶ ፡ ወሰደዶ ፡ እምብሔሩ ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወሰመየ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ነቢየ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ብራህም፤ ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ፋርስ ፡ ወሰጠቆ ፡ ፪ክፍለ ፡ ወነሥኦሙ ፡ ለ፪፻ ሰብእ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ተለውዎ ፡ ወቀበሮሙ"፣ ቀ.ልልቀሊተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሐቋሆሙ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ናሁ ፡ ተከልኩ ፡ አእዋደ፤' ፥ እምሰብእ ።
In the time of this father appeared in the land of the East a man by the name of Mäni (= Mani). This one wandered and called himself Paraclete, Spirit of justice. He came to the land of Soryä (= Syria); a righteous bishop by the name of 'Arkiläwos (= Archelaus) disputed with him, defeated him, revealed his error, and drove him from his land. He returned to the land of Färs (= Persia) and called himself a prophet. Berähem (= Behram), king of Färs, took him and (had him) split into two parts; he took two hundred of the people who followed him (= Mäni), (had them) buried head down up to the waist, and said: "Behold, I have planted an orchard of people."
ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አብ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፤ ወያዓቅቦሙ ፡ ለመርዔቱ ፡ ወያጸንዖሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ በተግሣጸት ፡ ወበድርሳናት ፣ ወያድኅኖሙ ፡ እምንዳቤ ፤ ከሐዲያን ፡ ወዕልዋን ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ አዕረፈ ። ወነበረ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
As for this father, he remained fighting (spiritually), guarding his flock, strengthening it in the right faith through exhortations and homilies, and preserving it from the persecution of the impious and the infidels until he died. He remained on the seat of Märgos the evangelist for seventeen years and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer for ever and ever, amen.

### READING FOR THE 15TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ቀዳሚት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘተቀደሰት፤ ፡ ለሕ ዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ያዕቆባዊያን ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ኒቆላዎስ" ፡ ኤስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ሚራ ። ዝውእቱ ፡ 8እምነ ፡ አበው ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ ኤኢስ ፡ ቆኦሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ኒቅያ ። ወተቀደሰት ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ፅውቅት ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the consecration of the first church that was consecrated for the people of the Jacobite Christians in the land of Egypt, in the name of the saint Niqoläwos (= Nicholas), bishop of the city of Mirä (= Myra); this one is one of the three hundred and eighteen bishop fathers who gathered in the city of Niqyä (= Nicaea). This church, known under (the


[p. 538] {#po.46.p538}
በቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሲኖዳ ፤ በምሥራቀ ፡ ሀገረ ፤ እስክንድርያ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወተአምነ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ መኳንንቲሁ ፡ ለዲዮቅልጢያኖስ ። ወበጽሖ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፡ ወኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወአትረፎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ወተጋብአ ፡ምስለ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ፻፻፲ ወ ፳። ወተአምራቲሁሰ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብዙኃት ፡ ፈድፋደ ፤ ወክሠታት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፤ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ አጋቦስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ እም ድወ፪አርድ እት ፡ ዘዘከሮ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ለጳውሎስ ፡ በዘ ፡ ይበጽሕ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዘተነበየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለቀላውድዮስ ፡ ቄሣር ። ወተፈጸመ ፡ ትንቢቱ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ወብድብድ ፤ ወአጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ። ወናሁ ፡፣ ጸሐፍነ ፡ ገድሎ ፤ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አመ ፡ ፪ለየካቲት ። ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
name of) the saint abbä Sinodä (= Shenoute), was consecrated to the east of the city of Alexandria. This saint was eminent; he confessed (Christ) before the officers of Diyogletiyänos (= Diocletian). Many misfortunes and torments happened to him, and God left him alive until he came to join the council of the three hundred and eighteen saints. As for the miracles of this saint, (they are) very numerous and known in all the ends of the world. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of the saint 'Agäbos (= Agabus) the apostle—who is (among) the seventy-two disciples—whom the book of the Acts of the Apostles mentions as having prophesied to Päwlos (= Paul) what would happen to him. It is he also who prophesied concerning the coming of the great famine that took place in the time of Qaläwdyos Qésär (= Claudius Caesar). His prophecy was fulfilled; the famine and the plague came and caused many people to perish. Behold, we have written the life of this saint on the 4th of Yakkatit. May his prayer be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ፀአተ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበ ረት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተዐውድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐየራት ፡ ፲ ወዓመተ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁራ" ፣ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነት ፡ ካዕበ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ እልስክንድራ" ፡፣ ሰማዕት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ለዶድያኖስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ሶበ ፡ ሰሐቀ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይወግር!‹ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ለአማልክቲሁ ፡ ወመሰሎ ፡ ለዶድያኖስ፤፤! ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙን ፡ ውእቱ ። ወተአዎምኃ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ "ወአብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ወጸ ለየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፤ ጊዮርጊስ!" ፡ ወአንበበ ፣ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ንግሥት ፡ እልስክንድራ፤ ። ወሰአለቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተርጉም ፡ ላቲ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወተርጐመ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ላቲ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወቦአ ፡ ቃሉ፤! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወአምነት ፡ በእግዚእ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተሳለቀ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዶድያኖስ፤፤ ፡ ወአጥፍአ ፡ ጣዖቶ ፡ ወአዘዛ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ወውሕተቶ ፡ ወተኃፍረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዶድያኖስ፤ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ እልስክንድራ፣፤" ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይቴክዝ ፡ ወየሐዝን፤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጊዮርጊስ ፡ አስተኃፈሮ ። ወትቤሎ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ኢይቤለከኑ ፡ ኢትትቃረኖሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ገሊላዊያን ፡ እስመ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ኅያል ፤ ወጽኑዕ ። ወተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዋ?፤ ፡ ወኩነንዋ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአት ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the departure of the soul of Yohannes (= John) the Baptist after it had remained seventeen years with his head which was moving in the air. May his holy blessing be with his dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.] On this day, there is also 'Eleskenderä (= Alexandra), the martyr, wife of Dodyänos (Julian?) the king. When the saint Giyorgis (= George) mocked Dodyänos for offering incense to his gods, it seemed to the latter that it was justified. He kissed the head of the saint Giyorgis and had him enter his royal palace. The saint Giyorgis prayed and read the psalms of Däwit (= David) before the queen 'Eleskenderä. She asked him to explain (them) to her himself; he explained (them) to her and expounded to her the divinity of our Lord Jesus Christ. His word entered her heart and she believed in the Lord Christ. When the saint Giyorgis mocked Dodyänos, destroyed his idols, and ordered the earth to swallow them, the king Dodyänos was ashamed and entered to the saint 'Eleskenderä his wife, grieving and sorrowing because the saint Giyorgis had covered him with shame. This saint said to him: "Did I not tell you not to oppose the Christians, the Galileans, for their God (is) powerful and strong?" (Dodyänos) became angry with her and ordered her to be tortured; she was subjected to great torture, her head was cut off with the sword, and she received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. May God have mercy on us through her prayer, amen and amen.


[p. 540] {#po.46.p540}

### READING FOR THE 16TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲፪ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፣ ቅዱስ ፡ አንቲቦስ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ኤሏስ ፡ ቆፅስ ፡ ዘሀገረ" ፣ በርዓመ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ በመለኮት ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለጦማቲያኖስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ አስባሲያኖስ ፣ ዘአመዝበረ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ኢየሩሳ ሌም ። ዝንቱ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ አጥፍኦሙ ፡ ለአይሁድ ፡ እመንግሥቱ ፡፡'ወኮነ ፡ ኢያስተርኢ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡፤ ግሙራ ። ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፣ እምውሉዶ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈርህ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ወመንግሥቱ ። ወሰምዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ ወእሙንቱ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወፈርሀ ፤ ወቀተሎሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወጐየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ በመለኮት ። ወፈነወ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወአብጽሖሙ ፡ ለውሉደ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ 'ወልደ ፡ ዮሴፍ፤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሱራን፤፤ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ሮሜ፤፤ ። ወሶበ ፤ ተስእሎሙ ፡፣ በእንተ ፤ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወአውሥእዎ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወክርስቶስኒ ፡ የኃ ድር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወውእቱ ፡ሀሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ በደኃሪ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይቤንን ፡ ሕያዋነ ፡ ወምውታነ ፡ ወይፈድዮሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ለለ፳8 በከመ ፡ ምግባሮሙ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፣ ወኃደጎሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ወኢኮነ ፡ ያመነድቦሙ ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያክብርዎሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ዝንቱ፤ ፡ ቅዱስ ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኩነኖ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወፅፁበ ፡ ከመ ፤ ያክሕዶ፤ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ተወሰከ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፤ ተአምኖተ ፡ ወሃይማኖተ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ረሰይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘብርት ፡፤ ወአንደዱ ፡ መትሕቴሁ ፤ እሳተ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሖ ፡ ወይቄድሶ ፡ ወያአኩቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘረሰዮ፤ ፡ ድልወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ኃሠሠ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡እለ ፡ ይዜከሩ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሶሙ!፤ ፡ ለዙሎሙ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ደዌሆሙ ፡ ዘነፍስ ፡ ወዘሥጋ ። ወፈጸመ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ 'በውስተ ፡ ውእቱ!" ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘብርት ። ወሶበ ፡ ገደፍዎ ፡ ከሐዲያን ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡፣ ነሥእዎ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወበዝማሬ ፡ ወበማኅሌት ፡ ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ! ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልእ ክት ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይናዝዞ ፡ "ውስቴታ ፡ ወያጸንዖ ፡ ወይሰምዮ" ፡ ካህነ ፡ ወመእመነ ፡ ወኖላዌ ፡ ጌረ ። ወሰመዮ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ወዓይድኦ፤፤ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፣፤ ተጐለቄ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያት" ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወሰማዕታት ። ወተብህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እረፍት ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ፈድፋዶ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በቀዔት ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ዘይፈቅዶ ፡ በአሚን ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint 'Antibos (= Antipas), bishop of the city of Bar'äma (= Pergamum), disciple of Yohannes (= John) the evangelist, the theologian. This saint lived in the time of Tomätiyänos (= Domitian), son of Asbäsiyänos (= Vespasian) who devastated the city of Jerusalem. This emperor made the Jews disappear from his kingdom: not a single Jew appeared anymore in his kingdom. He killed many sons of kings, fearing for himself and his empire. He learned that the Christians said that Christ (was) their god and (that) they were a very great race. He was afraid and killed many believers. Yohannes the evangelist, the theologian, fled far from him. (The emperor) dispatched to Jerusalem and had the sons of Yehudä (= Judah), son of Yosëf (= Joseph), brought in chains to the city of Romë (= Rome). When he questioned them about the kingdom of Christ, they answered him and told him that the kingdom of Christ was heavenly and (that) Christ dwelt in heaven: He would return on the last day, would judge the living and the dead, and would reward each according to his actions. When the emperor heard this statement, he was very afraid, released the Christians, did not persecute them (anymore), and ordered them to be honored. Among those who had become martyrs by his hand, there is this saint: he subjected him to numerous and painful tortures to make (him) deny Christ. But he (= the saint) confessed and believed all the more in the tortures. After that, they put him in a bronze bull and lit a great fire underneath. He glorified, proclaimed holy, and thanked God who had made him worthy to become a martyr for His holy name. Then he asked God, concerning all those who would mention his name and would make his commemoration, that He might heal them all of all diseases of the soul and of the body. He finished his martyrdom in the bronze bull. When the pagans threw away his body, the faithful took his holy body with great pomp, with chants and canticles, and placed it in a church. When he was in prison, a letter reached him from the saint Yohannes the evangelist where (the latter) comforted him, strengthened him, and called him priest, believer, and virtuous pastor. He called him martyr, taught him how he would become a martyr, and said to him: "You have been counted with the holy apostles and the martyrs." It is said that, until today, there flows from the body of the saint a very sweet perfume, useful to whoever wants it with faith. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 542] {#po.46.p542}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈት ፡ ወለተ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ወለተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ፡ ወተቀብረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ ዳጋ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
On this day also died Walatta Seyon, daughter of the king (illegible); she was buried in the sanctuary of Dägä. May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለሳባ ፡ ሰማዕት ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፤ ፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of Säbä the martyr. May his holy blessing be with his dear ‘Atnatéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 17TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲ሂለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡፤ ቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ እሁ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ዘብዴዎስ ። ወውእቱኬ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሰበከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አድያ፤ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ወመንክራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ ፡ እስከ ፡ አብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ልዑል ። ወእምዝ ፡፣ ወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አህጉር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወሰበከ ፡ ውስቴ ቶሙ ፡ በስብከተ ፡ ወንጌሉ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወጸውዖሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይእመኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወአዘ ዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቡ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ፍረያቲሆሙ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ወይመጽውቱ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወምስኪናን፤ ። ወኮነ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ነገድ ፡ ዝርዋን ፡ ይዜን ዎሙ ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ዘበአ ማን ። እስመ ፡ ኢኮኑ ፡ እሉ ፡ ነገድ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ዘያመልኩ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ነገድ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ጣዖታተ ፡ ዘኃረዩ ፡ ለርእሶሙ ፡ አማልክተ ፡ ወለኩሉ ፡ ግልፎ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡፣ ስሕተተ ፡ ያስሕትዎሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ እምታሕተ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡፤ እንዘ ፡ ይሁቡ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይረክቦሙ ፡ ወንዋየ ፡ ዘያመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡፤ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተዐቢ ፤ ሚመቱ ፡ ወትፈደፍድ ፣ መንግሥቱ ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint Yä'qob (= James) the apostle, the brother of Yohannes (= John), son of Zabdëwos (= Zebedee). This (took place) after he had preached in the land of 'Adyä (= India?) and had performed there numerous miracles and wonders to the point of making them (= the inhabitants) enter into the knowledge of God the Most High. Then he left toward many lands, preached there the gospel of Christ, called them (= the inhabitants) to believe in Him, and ordered them to give the first fruits of their crops to the Church and to give alms to the poor and (to the) needy. [When he left toward the twelve scattered tribes, he spoke to them of the peace of our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to Him!—our true God. Indeed, all these tribes did not worship God, but all (these) tribes had idols that they had chosen for themselves as gods, and each effigy of these led them into error. It was under the reign of Hërodes (= Herod), (the tribes) gave him their products as they reached them. The money that they brought him from their (goods) (constituted) much money, so that his situation grew and his royal power increased.
ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነገድ ፡ በልሳነ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ወአኮ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አላ ፡ ዘእንስሳ ፡ ወአራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአፅዋፈ ፡ ሰማይ ። ሶበ ፡ ይትናገሩ ፡ በልሳናቲሆሙ ፡ የአምር ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በረድኤተ ፡፣ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ይሰብክ ፣ በማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ወይኤዝዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጉ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እከየ ፤ ምግባሮሙ ፡ ወይእመኑ ፡ በእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሕያው ፡ ወበዋሕድ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፣፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማኅየዊ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፤ እለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መናፍስቲሆሙ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወዘይቤንኖሙ ፡ 'ለሙታን ፡ ወለሕያዋን ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢተሀቡ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየክሙ ፡ ለነገሥተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዳእሙ ፣ ሀቡ ፡ ለምስኪናን ፡፤ እምኔሃ ፣፡ ለመድኃኒተ ፡ ነፍስክሙ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ሰዐት ፡ ኃደረ ፡ ውስቴቶሙ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ፍርሃተ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውስተ ፡
As for Yä'qob, when he arrived among them, he preached to each tribe in the language of its country, for our Lord had given him the knowledge of all languages, not only the language of men, but (also) that of domestic animals, beasts of the desert, and birds of the sky. When they conversed in their language, the apostle understood all the words thanks to the help of the Holy Spirit. Yä'qob preached among them and ordered them to abandon their evil actions and to believe in the living God, in His only Son our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to Him!—and (in) the life-giving Holy Spirit for all creatures whose every vital breath (is) in His hand and who will judge the dead and the living. He said to them: "Do not give all your goods to the kings of the earth, but give some to the needy for the salvation of your soul." At that moment, the grace of the Holy Spirit dwelt in them and the fear of God entered their hearts. The good news
አልባቢሆሙ ። ወተሰብከ ፡ ዜና ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ በነገረ፤፣ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወጸንዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሚን ፡ በእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ንጉሠ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ዘኢይሜንኖሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡፤ የኃሥሥዎ ፡ ወይትመየጡ ፡፤ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምሕሊና፤፤ ፡ ርቱዕ ። ውእቶሙኒ ፡ መነንዎሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፤እለ ፡ የአምኑ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ወግብሮሙ ፡ እኩየ ፡ ዘይገብሩ ፡ ኃደጉ ፡ ወተመይጡ፣፤ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በርቱዕ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ወተመጠዉ ፡ ነገሮ ፡'ለቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ፤" ፡ እአለ፣ ዜነዎሙ ፡ ቦቱ ። 'ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ያዕቆብ፤ ፡ አፍቀሮሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ በእንተ" ፡ ዘአፍጠኑ፤፤ ፤ ተመጥዎ ፡ ስብከቱ ፡ ወኃደጉ ፡ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡፤ ብዙኃ ፡ አስሕቶ ። ወአፍጠነ ፡ ቅዱስ፤" ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ወኀነፀ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ውስተ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርቲሆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነጸረ ፡ ስነ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፣ ወአጥመቆሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ተፈሥሑ ፡ 'ወተኃሠዩ ፡ ኅቡረ፤ ። ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡እስእለክሙ ፡ አነ ፡ ከመ ፡የሀቡ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነገድ ፡ መባአ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፤ ቀዳሜ፤ ፤ አቅማሕክሙ!" ፡ ወዐፀደ ፡ ወይንክሙ ፡ ወእንስሳክሙ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ለነዳያን ። ወአውሥእዎ፣ ሕዝብ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ንፈቅድ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአዘዝከነ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ነገድ ፡ እምቀዳሚያተ ፡ ኩሉ ፤" ፡ ጥሪቶሙ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ።
was preached in all their lands: (the people) believed in the word of Yä'qob the apostle and were strengthened in the belief in our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to Him!—King of heaven and earth, who does not despise those who seek Him and turn to Him with a right mind. They despised all (the idols) in which they had believed, they abandoned the evil actions they were doing, turned to God with a right mind, and welcomed the words of the saint Yä'qob by which he announced the good news to them. As for the saint Yä'qob, he loved them much, because they had welcomed his preaching quickly and had abandoned those who were doing much deception. The saint Yä'qob the apostle quickly built churches for them in all their lands. When he observed the beauty of their faith, he baptized them in the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit. They rejoiced and exulted together. He prescribed to them the commandments of the Gospel and the law of the faith and said to them: "I ask you that each tribe give as an offering to the Church the first fruits of your harvests, of your vineyards, and of your domestic animals so that they may be for God (as) food for the poor." The people answered him and said to him: "We want to do everything that you will order us," and the tribe brought to the Church the first fruits of all its goods.]


[p. 544] {#po.46.p544}
ወኮኑ፤፤! ፡ እምታሕተ ፡ ስልጣኑ ፡ ለሄሮድስ ፡ ንጉሥ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ይኤዝዝ። ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ፈድፋዶደ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አንተኑ ፡ ዘአዘዝከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየ ሀቡ ፡ ጸባሕተ ፡ ለቄሣር ፡ ወኢየሀቡ ፡ አምኃ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ 'አላ ፤ አዘዝኮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቡ ፡ ንዋዮሙ ፡ ለምጽወት ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወለነዳያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተምዓ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወዘበጦ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወመተረ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ ዘኢይማስን ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወኮነ ፡ ሁከት ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ በኢየሩሳሌም ። ወነ ሥኦ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወሞቅሖ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ይሔሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ፋሲካ ፡ ይቅትሎ ። ወመልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔርሰ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ለሄሮድስ" ፡ ወሞተ ፡ በእኩይ ፡ ሞት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ በድኑ ፡ ጽዩእ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰብሐ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወኢሰብሖ ፡ ለአምላኩ ። ወሥጋሁሰ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወገነዝዎ ፣፤ ወቀበ ርዎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መቅደስ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡
They were under the dominion of Hërodes the king. When this one heard about this saint Yä'qob the apostle that he was giving this order, he became very angry, dispatched, had him brought, and said to him: "Is it you who ordered that they should not give tribute to Qésär (= Caesar) and should not give a gift to the king—but you ordered them to give their money as alms to the Church and to the poor?" Then he became angry with him, struck him with his hand with a sword, and cut off his head. (Yä'qob) received the unfading crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. There was great trouble in Jerusalem. (Hërodes) took Pêtros (= Peter), the head of the apostles, and imprisoned him; he was thinking of killing him after the feast of the Passover. But the angel of God struck Hërodes and (the latter) died a bad death. His body became putrid when he glorified himself and did not glorify his God. As for the body of the saint Yä'qob the apostle, believers took it, shrouded it, and buried it near the Temple. May God have mercy on us through his prayer and may his blessing be with us.
ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ፅለት ፤ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአንዮስ ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡፤ ንጉሥነ ፡ ፋሲለ ደስ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of 'Anyos. May his blessing be with our king Fäsiladas for ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 18TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፲፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ክቡር ፤ አውሳብዮስ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint (and) illustrious 'Awsäbyos (= Eusebius),

the slave of the saint Susänyos (= Sisennius). As Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) (was) torturing the saint Susänyos, the father of Susänyos denounced this saint (= 'Awsäbyos) to the emperor and said to the latter: "Susänyos has a slave by the name of 'Awsäbyos; this one worships Christ as his master and does not worship your gods." The emperor ordered him to be brought. When he had arrived before him, he questioned him about his faith. (The saint) confessed before him our Lord Christ and His divinity. The emperor made him many promises and reprimanded him for having abandoned the worship of his (= the emperor's) gods. The saint reprimanded the emperor for having abandoned the worship of the Lord, the true God. The emperor redoubled in fury and anger and ordered the head of this saint to be cut off before his master. They cut off his head with the sword and he received the crown of martyrdom. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.

[On this day also, abbä Pêtros (= Peter), the relative of abbä Besoy (= Bsoi) the blessed, became a martyr. The name of the father of this saint was Sidräq (= Sidracos) and the name of his mother, Märyäm (= Mary). When they had begotten this saint, Kiros, the sister of his mother, took him and raised him with her son abbä ‘Ebsoy. When he was seven years old, they were led to a teacher and they learned all the law of the Church. They remained in fasting and in prayer, performing miracles and wonders. The angel of God Gabr'ël (= Gabriel) appeared to them and told them that they would become martyrs for our Lord Christ—glory to Him! A short time later, Satan seduced Diyogletyänos (= Diocletian) the impious so that he would worship idols. These saints were denounced as being Christians to 'Aryänos (= Arianus), the governor of 'Ensenä (= Antinoe). (The latter) summoned them before him and (wanted) to force them to worship the idols. As they refused (it) to him, he ordered them to be hoisted on a rack until their blood flowed to the ground. For the blessed Pêtros, the pain of the torture was manifest from the fact that his body was weakened by the rigor of fasting and prayer. The angel of God came then and healed him of his pain. When 'Aryänos saw this thing, he ordered abbä Pêtros to be taken to the east of the city and his head to be cut off with the sword. While abbä 'Ebsoy was in prison, the angel of God took him and brought him to his relative Pêtros. They took each other in their arms and embraced. Then they prayed to God to reunite them in the heavenly Jerusalem. Behold, a voice came to them from heaven, which said: "Greetings to you, (spiritual) combatants, for you will be in the midst of the assembly of heavenly beings and your bodies will not be separated on earth!" Then the angel of God took abbä 'Ebsoy and brought him back into the prison. The soldiers cut off the head of the victorious hero abbä Pêtros and hung his body on a tree. Then abbä 'Ebsoy asked the prison guard to let him go in secret. (Once) gone, he bought the body of abbä Pêtros,


[p. 548] {#po.46.p548}
በአፈዋት ፡ ወቀበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ኅቡእ ። በረከቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፤ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካሮሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ጸድቃን፤፤ ፡ ሰማዕታት ፡ እለ ፡ ተኩነኑ፣ በውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ጠርሶስ" ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሮሙ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
shrouded it with perfumes, and buried it in a hidden place. May his holy blessing be with his dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.] [On this day (also, there is) the commemoration of the saints (and) righteous martyrs who were tortured in the city of Tarsos (= Tarsus). May their prayer and their blessing be with their dear ‘Atnätéwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 19TH OF MIYAZYA

In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint Sem'on (= Simeon), who (is) from the land of 'Armänyä (= Armenia), bishop of the land of Färs (= Persia), and, with this one, one hundred and fifty martyrs. This saint lived in the kingdom of Säbor (= Sapor), son of Hermaz (= Hormez), who was called "shoulder-tearer," for, when he defeated another king, he would chain him and tear off his shoulders. This pagan subjected the believers to a great persecution and reduced them to severe servitude. This saint sent him a letter in which he said to him: "Those whom our Lord Christ has redeemed by His glorious blood are exempt from the servitude of men and are to our Lord Christ alone. Because of this, they do not deserve servitude and are not forced (to pay) a tribute beyond the status that has been fixed for them. But they choose to die for Christ who shed His blood for them and not to work for the impious who have transgressed the law." When Säbor read the letter of this saint, he became very angry, (had) him bound with two iron chains, and had him brought and thrown into prison. This saint found in the prison many people who were pagans and worshipped the sun. They were incarcerated for another reason (than him). The saint exhorted them and instructed them; they believed in our Lord Christ, their heads were cut off, and they received the crown of martyrdom. After that, the king had this saint brought from the prison, one hundred and fifty men with him. This saint strengthened them until their heads were cut off, and they received the crown of martyrdom. One of them trembled, was afraid of the sword, and wanted to abjure. One of those who were standing (there) strengthened him and said to him: "Do not tremble, for a blow of the sword (is) as nothing if you close your eyes." (The man) did so and received the crown of martyrdom. The man who had strengthened him was denounced, his name (was) Basig. The king had him brought, (had) his tongue cut off and his skin torn off, and he gave up the ghost. After that, (the king) had this saint Sem'on brought and said to him: "If you do not worship the gods, I will subject you to great torture." The saint did not obey his order and was not


[p. 550] {#po.46.p550}
ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወቅዱስሰ ፡ ኢሰምዓ ፡ ትእዛዙ ፡ ወኢፈርሀ ፡ እምኩነኔሁ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምትሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በሰይፍ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ፡ በመንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወኮነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ" ፡ ፻፳ ወ፪ዓመት! ። እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አሜን ።
afraid of his torture. (The king) then ordered his head to be cut off; they cut off his head with the sword and he received the crown of martyrdom in the kingdom of heaven. All his days were one hundred and twenty-seven years. May God have mercy on us through the prayer of this (saint), amen.
በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወዓሌ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ርቱዓ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ወተቀብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቅደሰ ፡ዳጋ ።
On this day died Sarda Krestos, the guard of the king of right faith Sarda Dengel; he was buried in the sanctuary of Däga.

### READING FOR THE 20TH OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፳ለሚያገዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በብኑዳ፤ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ ደንደራ ፣ ወትሰመይ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በንደራ፤ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ባሕታዌ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጸድቀ ፡ ወመስተጋድለ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ልበስ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ወረድ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ፆማዕት ፡ ወአስተርአይ ፡ ለመኩንን ። ወኮነ ፡ አርያኖስ ፡ መኩንን ፡ በጽሐ ፤ ውስተ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ኅበ ፡ መርሶ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወኀሠሦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ወኢረ ከቦ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በቡኑዳ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ወጸርኃ ፡ በቃል ፡ ልዑል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አነ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ገሃደ ፡ አአምን ፡ በእግዚእየ ፡ አየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ባሕታዊ ፡ ዘዕውቅ ፡ ዘኅኀሠሦ ፡ ወኢረከቦ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንንዎ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወኩነንዎ ፡ ወሞቅሕዎ ፡ በሐጺን ፡ ወወደ ይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤት ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ወሠረቀ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ሰማያዊ ። ወአስተርአዮ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወፈወሶ ፡ እምሕማማቲሁ ፡ ወናዘዞ ። ወሀሎ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ብእሲ ፡ መሃይምን ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቄርሎስ ፡ ወምስሌሁ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወወለቱ ፡ ወ፲ወ፪ወራዙቱ ። ለእሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መሀሮሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአጽንዖሙ ፡ ወመተሩ ፡ አርእስቲሆሙ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሰማዕተ ። ወተምዓ ፡ መኩንን ፡ ወአዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስቅሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እብነ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወይውግርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ወበኃይለ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸበተ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እብን ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ይስቅልዎ?" ፡ 'ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲበ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ወፈረየት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በቀልት ፡ ፲ወ፪አስካለ ፤ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ። ወእምድኅረዝ" ፡ መጠወ ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ፤" ፡ ነፍሶ፤!፤ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ አክሊለ ፡ ስምዕ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day became a martyr the saint Babnudä (= Paphnuce) who (was) from the city of Dandarä (= Dendera); (this one) is also called Bandarä. This saint was a solitary, he was righteous and a (spiritual) combatant. The angel of God appeared to him and said to him: "Put on the clothes of the liturgical office, descend from this cell, and appear before the governor." 'Aryänos (= Arianus) the governor had arrived by boat at the port of the city, had searched for the solitary, and had not found him. This saint Babunudä came to him of his own free will and cried out with a loud voice saying: "I am a Christian openly, I believe in my Lord Jesus Christ." When (the governor) knew that it was the well-known solitary whom he had searched for and had not found, he ordered him to be subjected to great torture. He was tortured, he was bound with (chains of) iron, and he was thrown into a dark dungeon. A heavenly light shone upon him; the angel of God appeared to him, healed him of his sufferings, and consoled him. There was in the city a believing man by the name of Qérellos (= Cyril) and, with him, his wife, his daughter, and his twelve young sons. The saint instructed them all and strengthened them; their heads were cut off and they became martyrs. The governor became angry and ordered [a large stone to be hung from the neck of this saint and him to be thrown into the river; by the power of God, the saint floated with the stone. (The governor) ordered also] this saint to be hung from a palm tree: the palm tree immediately gave twelve clusters (of dates) and it has (remained) in memory (of that) until this day. After that, this saint gave up the ghost and received the crown of martyrdom. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፤ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አሞኒ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸ ሎቱ ፡ ለንጉሥነ ፡ ፋሲለደስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the commemoration of abbä 'Amoni. May God have mercy through his prayer upon our king Fäsiladas for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 21ST OF MIYAZYA
አመ ፡ ፳፳ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተዝካረ ፡ በዓላ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወላ ዲተ ፡ አምላክ ። እስመ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ትተነብል፤ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወልዳ ፤ ወታስተሰሪ ፣ ኃጣውኢሆምጮ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጴውዑ ፡ ስማ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይደልወነ ፡ ናብዕል ፡ ላቲ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ተዝካራ ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፣ ብሩታዎስ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ ማእምራን ፡ ወመማክርት ፡ ወጠቢባን ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቴና ። ወኮነ ፡፤ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአረጋዊያን ፡ መምህራን ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ደራስያን ። ወተጋብአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐዋርያ" ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ወተዋሥኡ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወተከሥተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ርትዕት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፤ ክርስቶስ ። ወአምነ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ለሐዋርያ ፡፤ ጳውሎስ ፣፤ ወአጥመቆ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ወሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቴና ። ወተጋብአ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዲዮናስዮስ ፡ አብሩባጊታስ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ማእምራነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ አቴና ፡ ካዕበ ። ወከሠተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሃይማኖተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወተርጐመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ዘኮነ ፡ ስዉረ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ማእምረ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ብሩታዎስ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፣ ቅድስት ፡ ድንግል ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወቆመ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣፤ ወናዘዞሙ ፡ በዝማሬያት ፡ ወበማኅሌታት”" ፡ ዘደረሰ ፡ በመኃ ልይ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ በዜማ ፡ ውሱቅ ፡ ጥዑም ። ወሜጦሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ እምነ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ ወአሕዛብ ፡ ወአብኦሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወነገደ ፡ በጸጋ ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወረብሐ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ረብሐ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ከመ፣፤ ይሚምዎ ፡ ኤጺስ ፡ ቆጾፅሰ ፡ ወሰአሎሙ ፣፤ ወአስተብቀዓ ፣ ኀቤሆሙ ፤ ወይቤሎሙ ፥ መሐሩኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ ኢይደሉ፤" ፡ ለዛቲ ፤ ሚመት ፡ ክብርት ፡ ወኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ሕገ፤፤ ፡ ክህነት ፡ ዘቀሲስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ በአእምሮቱ ፡ ወበምግባሩ ፡ ወበትምህርቱ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡፤ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day, (there is) the commemoration of the feast of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary, Mother of God. [Indeed, she intercedes for the children of men before her beloved Son and asks for forgiveness for the sins of those who invoke her name. Because of this, it is fitting that we celebrate for her the day of her commemoration.] May her intercession, her prayer, and her blessing be with us for ever, amen. On this day also died the holy father Berutäwos (= Proteus). This illustrious (saint) was one of the learned, prudent, and wise men who (were) in the city of 'Atënä (= Athens). He was among the old doctors who (were) at the school (of Athens). He met with the apostle Päwlos (= Paul) and they disputed together concerning the faith. It was revealed to him that there was no right faith except the faith of our Lord Jesus Christ. He believed through the intermediary of the apostle Päwlos; (the latter) baptized him with the Christian baptism and taught him the rule and the law of the Church. After that, he laid his hands on him and ordained him priest of the city of 'Aténä. This saint also met with the saint Diyonäsyos 'Abrubägitäs (= Dionysius the Areopagite), the head of the learned of the city of 'Atënä. He expounded to him the faith of our Lord Christ and explained to him what is hidden in it, and (Diyonäsyos) became very learned. This saint Berutäwos came (to Jerusalem?) on the day of the death of our Lady the holy Virgin Mary; he stood in the midst of the apostles and consoled them with chants and canticles that he had composed by singing (them) with his mouth with a harmonious (and) sweet accompaniment. He converted many Jews and Gentiles and made them enter into the knowledge of our Lord Christ. He traded with the grace that had been given to him and drew great profit (from it). When the apostles wanted to consecrate him bishop, he prayed to them, beseeched them, and said to them: "Have mercy on me, for I do not deserve this glorious office and I will not be able to follow the priestly law of the priest." When he had received the heavenly grace through his science, through his actions, and through his teaching, he went to God to whom (belongs) the glory for ever. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፣ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስምዖሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡'አኮርክስ ፡ ወይቅሪጾስ ፡ ወዮራስ፤ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐሮ ፡ በጸሎቶሙ!፤ ፡ ለፍቁሮሙ!፤ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
[On this day also, (there is) the martyrdom of the saints 'Akorkes, Yegrisos, and Yoräs. May God have mercy through their prayer upon His dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]


[p. 554] {#po.46.p554}
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ክቡር ፡ በዐለ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወሰባኬ ፣ ሃይማኖት ፤ አምሳሊሆሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ አባ ፡ እንባቆም ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምርጎቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ እስከ ፡ ምኔቱ፤፤ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዘዐጥመቆ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ክርስትና ፡ ወወሀቦ ፡ ለመምህር ፤ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፣ ምንኩስና ። ወተጋደለ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ገድል ፡ ሠናይ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደለዎ ፡ ይሠየም ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወኮነ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ መምህራን ፡ ፲ወ፳ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈለሰ ፡ በርስዕ ፥ ሠናይ ፡ ኀበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘአፍቀሮ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ ምንዳቤ ፤ ወጻማ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወስደተ ። ረድኤተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍቁሩ ፡ አትናቴዎስ ፡
[On this day (also), there is the death of the illustrious father, who left a great memory, the preacher of the faith, the image of the apostles, abbä 'Enbägom (= Habakkuk). God called this saint from a distant land to the monastery of our father Takla Häymänot, in the time of our father Pêtros (= Peter); it is this one who baptized him with his hand with the Christian baptism. He entrusted him to a teacher and, after that, clothed him with the monastic habit. (‘Enbägom) gave himself to every beautiful (spiritual) combat so that he deserved to be named to the seat of our father Takla Häymänot. Among the abbot fathers, he is the eleventh. Then, in a beautiful old age, he went to God who loved him after having endured many misfortunes and trials and exile. May the help of his prayer be with his dear ‘Atnätëwos for ever and ever, amen.]

### READING FOR THE 22ND OF MIYAZYA
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። አመ ፡ ፳፪ለሚያዝያ ፡ ምንባብ ። በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ አምላክ ። በዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ዘእምሀገረ ፡ ሆሬን፤ ፡ እምደወለ ፡ ሰባስ፤ ፡ እምደቡበ ፡ ግብጽ ። ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ንጹሓን ፡ ወስሙ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወስማ ፡ ለእሙ ፡ ሱስና ። ወአዕረፈት ፡ እሙ ፡፣ ወተረፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐፅኖ ። ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ኮነ ፡ የዓቅብ ፡ አባግዓ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወአውሰበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ካልእተ ፡ ብእሲተ ። ወበእማንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኮነ ፡ ረኃብ ፡ ዓቢይ ። ወኮነት ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ትጸልኦ ፡ ወትሁቦ፤ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ንስቲተ ። ወኮነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ 'ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ እስከ ፡ ምሴት ። ወኮነ ፡ መዋዕሊሁ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፳ዓመት ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ ኩሎ ፡ አሚረ ፣ ወይጸውም ፡ እስከ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ወሖረ!" ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይ ። ወሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ቅዱስ፤፤ ፡ አሰረ ፤ ውስተ ፡ አጽፉ ፡ ፻ጽቡረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሰሎ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ሶበ ፤ ይሬእዮሙ ፡ እሱራን ፡ ከመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ኅብስት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወፈትሐ ፡ ዓጽፎ ፡ ወረከቦሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ ጽቡር ፡ ከዊኖሙ ፤" ፡ ኅብስተ ። ወተስእሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ 'ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ወሀበ፤" ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ አው ፡ አልቦ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ለኖሎት ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ብዙ ኃን ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ስምዓ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ወአንከረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወሰብሐ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ።
In the name of the Father and of the Son and of the Holy Spirit, one God. On this day died the holy father Yeshaq (= Isaac), who was from the city of Horën (= Hourin) in the district of Sabäs (= Chabäâs) in Lower Egypt. The parents of this saint were virtuous; his father's name was 'Abrehäm (= Abraham) and his mother's name was Susennä (= Suzanne). His mother died and he remained with his father, who raised him. When he had grown up, he began to tend his father's sheep. His father married another woman. At that time, there was a great famine; his father's wife hated him and gave him only a little bread. This saint gave the bread—his own food—to the shepherds every day, and as for himself, he fasted until evening. At that time, he was five years old. When his father learned that his son gave the bread—his own food—to the shepherds every day and fasted until evening, his father went to see him. When the saint knew this, he fixed three balls of clay in his tunic so that, when he saw them, his father would imagine they were bread. When his father arrived near him, he opened his (the child's) tunic and found that the balls of clay had become bread. He asked the one who had told him the fact whether the child had given his bread that day to the shepherds or not. The man said to him: "Yes, he gave his food to the shepherds," and many people came and testified to this. His father was astonished and glorified God.
ወሶበ ፡ ልህቀ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ወመንኩሰ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ፳ብእሲ ፡ ጸድቅ ፡ ዘስሙ፣ ኤልያስ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ። ወሶበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አባ ፡ ኤልያስ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በርኑግ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ በኅበ ፡ ፳ አረጋዊ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ አባ፤ ፡ ዘካርያስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ይጸመድ ፡ ወይትጋደል ፡ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ። ወኮነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ የዐውድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡
When this saint had grown up, he went and became a monk under a righteous man named 'Élyäs (= Elijah); he remained with him for many years. When abbä 'Élyäs died, Yeshaq went to the mountain of Barnug (= Pernoudj) and remained with an old man named abbä Zakäryäs (= Zacharias); he devoted himself to piety and engaged in a great spiritual struggle.


[p. 556] {#po.46.p556}
በሓውርት ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከቦ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ በር ኑግ ፡ ወረከቦ ፡ በህየ ፡ ሰአሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወአበዮ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፣ ዘካርያስ ፡ ሖር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡከ ፡ ወንበር ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ የዓርፍ ። ወሖረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወኢነበረ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ ሕዳጥ ፣ መዋዕል ፡ "ወአፅረፈ ፤ አቡሁ!፤ ። ወወሀበ ፤ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ዘኀደገ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለነዳያን ፡ ወለምስኪናን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሐነጸ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መካነ ፡ ርጐቅ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ወተጸምደ ፡ ወተጋደለ ፤ ተጋድሎ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ በጾም ፡ ወበጸሎት ፡ ወበትጋህ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዓት ፡ እስከ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ወተረ ሥዓ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ።
His father traveled throughout the whole country to find him. When his father arrived at the mountain of Barnug and found him there, he asked him to return with him; the saint refused him. Abbä Zakäryäs said to him: "Go with your father and remain with him until he dies." This saint went with his father, but he remained with him for only a short time, and his father died. He gave all the goods his father had left to the poor and the needy. Then he built himself a cell away from the city, lived alone, devoted himself to piety, and engaged in a great spiritual struggle in fasting, prayer, and continuous, ceaseless vigil, until he died in peace. He was placed in a spot, and that spot was forgotten.
ወእምድኅረ ፤ ብዙኅ ፤ ዓመታት ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥቶ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበርህ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ መቃብሪሁ ፡ ለሰብእ?" ፡ እለ ፡ የአርሩ ፤ እክለ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፤ ተአምረ ፣፤ መጠነ ፡ ፻ጀመዓልት ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርአይዎ ፡ ይትኀባእ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ። ወአስተርአየ!" ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይበርህ ፣፤ መልዕልተ ፤ መቃብሪሁ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ አስተርአየ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በሕልም ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወአይድዖሙ ፡ መካነ፤ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወነሥእዎ ፡ በክብር ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወጸዓንዎ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ገመል ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ እስከ ፡ አመ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሆሪን ፡ ሀገሩ ፡ ወማእከለ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ነስረት፤ ። ወአስተብረከ ፡ ገመል ፡ በህየ ፤ ወኢተንሥአ ፡ ወዘበጥዎ ፡ 'ብዙኃ ፡ ዝብጠተ ፡ ወኢተንሥአ ፡ ወአእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ፤! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሕንጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በህየ"። ፡ መካነ ። ወአንበርዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ወሐነጹ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ" ፡ ወአንበሩ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡
After many years, God wished to reveal him: a lamp shining above his tomb appeared to some people who were harvesting wheat. When they had seen this miracle for three days, they were very astonished. When they arrived at that spot to see it, the lamp was hidden from them until the story spread: the spot appeared to them and they found the lamp shining above the saint's tomb. Then the saint appeared in a dream to some believers and indicated to them the place where his body was. They took it with great honor, loaded it onto a camel, and walked until they reached the area between his city of Horin and the city of Nasrat (= Nachart). The camel knelt there; they struck it many times, but it did not rise. They recognized that it was the will of God that they should build him a dwelling there. They laid him in that place, built a church for him, and deposited his body there. Many miracles and wonders occurred there. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ተአምራት ፡ ወመንክራት ፡ ብዙኃት ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይምሐረነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ። ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡፤ እስክንድሮስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳ ሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፣ ወውእቱ ፤ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ፲ወ፪ ። ዘይቤ ፤ በእንቲአሁ ፡ አብ ፡፤ አትናቴዎስ ፣ ሐዋርያዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ አቡየ ፡ እልስክንድሮስ ፡ ኢያነብብ ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ አላ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ያነብብ ፤ ወብርሃን ፡ ይሄሉ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ። ወዘከረ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ መጽኣ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ አርእስተ ፡ መበለታት ፡ ወነገራሁ ፡እንዘ ፡ ይብላ ፡ ሀለዋ"" ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ደናግል ፡ ይጸውማ ፡ ጂመዋዕለ ፣፤ ወኢይገብራ ፡ ምንተኒ ፣፤ በእደዊሆን ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አኃውየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢጾምኩ ፡ ግሙራ ፡ ፪ዕለተ ፡ ወኢበላዕኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ እበልዕ ፡ በዓ ቅም ፡ ወእነውም ፡ በዓቅም ። ወንግራሆን፤ ፡ ከመ ፡፣ ይብልዓ ፡ በዓቅም ፡ ወይጹማ ፡ በዓቅም ፡ ወይትገበራ ፡ በዓቅም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ኮነ፣ እምሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ አበዊሁ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፤ ወመሃይምናነ፤ ። ወልህቀ ፣ በውስተ ፡ መልእክተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወተሐጽነ ፡ ባቲ ፡ እስመ ፡ አባ ፡ መክሲሞስ ፡ ሜሞ ፡ አናጐንስጢስ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ቴዎናስ ፡ ጫሞ ፡ ዲያቆነ ፡ ወአባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä 'Eskenderos (= Alexander), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; he is the nineteenth in the number of the patriarch fathers. It is he of whom the father 'Atnätëwos (= Athanasius) the Apostolic says: "My father 'Eleskenderos did not read the Gospel while sitting, but he read standing, and a light was before him." He also makes mention of him and says: "The heads of the nuns came to him and spoke to him, saying: 'There are virgins among us who fast for seven days and do nothing with their hands.' He said to them: 'My brothers, as for me, I have never fasted for two days, nor have I refrained from eating while the sun is visible; I eat with measure and I sleep with measure. You, my sisters, tell them to eat with measure, to fast with measure, and to work with measure in every good deed.'" This saint was from the city of Alexandria; his parents were Christian and believers. He grew up in the service of the Church and was raised there; indeed, abbä Maksimos (= Maximus) ordained him reader, abbä Tëwonäs (= Theonas) ordained him

ሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወኮነ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ወድንግለ ፡ እምንእሱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ፤ ፣ እስክንድሮስ" ፡ ወአርኪላስ" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ሞቅሕ ። ወሰአልዎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፍትሖ ፡ እምግዘት ፡ እስመ ፡ አርዮስ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰአሎሙ ፡ ለክልኤሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስአልዎ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰአልዎ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ አውገዞ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ በቅድሜሆሙ ፡ ወአይድፆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ከመቶ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ 'አስተርአየ ፡፤ ሎቱ ፡ 'ወአዘዘ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወነገሮሙ-፥" ፡ ከመ ፡ አርኪ ላስጾ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ አርኪላስ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ እስክንድሮስ ። ወነገሮሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡፣ ነገረ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ካህናተ" ፡ ሀገረ፥፤ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፤ ወአዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትወከፍዎ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወኢይሳተፉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ አባ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፤ ስምዖ ፣ ተሠይመ ፡ አርኪላስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፣ እምድኅሬሁ ፡ ወተዐደወ ፡ ወተወክፎ ፡ ለአርዮስ ፡ ወሜሞ ፡ ቀሲሰ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኢነበረ ፡ አርኪላስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ 5፪አውራኅ ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ተሠይመ፥፡ ፡
deacon, and abbä Pêtros (= Peter) ordained him priest. He was pure and chaste from his childhood. When the time approached for abbä Pêtros to become a martyr, this father 'Eskenderos and 'Arkiläs (= Achillas) entered to see him while he was in prison. They asked him to release 'Aryos (= Arius) from his excommunication, for 'Aryos had asked them both to intercede with abbä Pêtros on his behalf. When they interceded for him, he excommunicated him again in their presence. He taught them and told them that our Lord Jesus Christ had appeared to him and commanded him this. He told them that 'Arkiläs would be patriarch after him, and after 'Arkiläs, this father 'Eskenderos. He told them these things before the priests of the city of Alexandria and ordered them not to receive 'Aryos and not to enter into communion with him. When abbä Pêtros had accomplished his martyrdom, 'Arkiläs was consecrated patriarch after him. He disobeyed, received 'Aryos, and ordained him priest. Because of this, 'Arkiläs lasted only six months and died. After him, this father 'Eskenderos was consecrated.

When he was seated on his patriarchal throne, the leaders of the people came to him and asked him to receive 'Aryos; he refused and increased the excommunication. He said: "The father abba Pêtros ordered me and 'Arkiläs not to receive 'Aryos: he spoke to us and told us that our Lord Jesus Christ had excommunicated him; he gave us an order and said to us: 'Beware, do not receive 'Aryos and do not enter into communion with him.' When 'Arkiläs disobeyed and received 'Aryos, God removed him quickly from his office; as for me, I will not receive him." After that, he drove out 'Aryos and the people who believed in his evil faith. 'Aryos went to Quastantinos (= Constantine) the emperor, slandered this saint, and said to him: "Abba 'Eskenderos has excommunicated me unjustly." The emperor gathered for him the holy council of the three hundred and eighteen in the city of Niqyä (= Nicaea), and this father was the head of this council. He fought 'Aryos and revealed his heresy with words brief in expression and magnificent in their grandeur. He excommunicated him and did not receive his word. He pronounced the profession of faith, which God uttered through his mouth, with all the fathers; he established the rules, laws, sanctions, and regulations that are in the hands of all Christian peoples to this day. He established the fast and the feast of Easter and returned to his throne, victorious and joyful. He guarded his flock in grace and peace. He remained on the throne of Märqos (= Mark) the Evangelist for sixteen years and died in peace. May God have mercy on us through his prayer, amen.
ወበዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ዘሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እምጐልቆሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ሊቃነ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ሣወዘ ። ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እምሰብአ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ። ወኮነ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ንጹሕ ፡
On this day also died the holy father abbä Märqos (= Mark), patriarch of the city of Alexandria; he is the forty-ninth in the number of the patriarch fathers. This father was from the people of the city of Alexandria. He was a virgin, pure,


[p. 560] {#po.46.p560}
ወማእምር ፡ ክቡር ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ዳግማይ ፡ በስመ ፡ ማርቆስ ። ወሜሞ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ፣ ዲያቆነ ። ወኮነ ፡ ካህነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወሣእስአ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ወጥዑም ፡ ቃሉ ። ወበእንተ ፡ አእምሮቱ ፡ በአንብቦ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡እለ ፡ ይሰምዕዎ፣ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንበሮ ፡ አባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፤ ወመጠዎ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ "ኩሎ ፡ ሥርዓተ ፡ ወግብረቶ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጵጵስና ። ወኮነ ፡ ኢይገብር ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በምክሩ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አልበሶ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አባ፣ መቃርስ ። ወበይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንተ" ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ባቲ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ መጽአ ፡ 8ጻድቅ ፡ እምነ ፡ አረጋዊያን ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወተናገረ ፡ በቅድመ ፣ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ሀለዎ ፡ ወይደልዎ" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዕረፍቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ነገሮሙ ፡ ለኤሏስ ፡ ቆአሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሚምዎ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ። ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሜምዎ" ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳተ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ጐየ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡
learned, and honored. He is the second of the name of Märqos. Abbä Yohannes (= John) ordained him deacon. He was a very good priest; his mouth was eloquent, and his voice was sweet. Because of his knowledge in the reading of the Scriptures, all those who heard him rejoiced because of him. Then abbä Yohannes installed him in his house and entrusted him with all the administration and affairs of the patriarchate: he did nothing without his counsel. Then he clothed him with the monastic habit in the monastery of abbä Magäres (= Macarius). The day he donned the monastic habit, a righteous man among the holy elders came, spoke before all the people, and said: "This deacon by the name of Märqos is destined—he is worthy of it—to sit on the throne of his father Märqos the Evangelist." When the time of his death was near, abbä Yohannes told the bishops to consecrate this father. They rejoiced at it and consecrated him patriarch against his will after he had fled far from them to the desert of 'Asqêtes (= Scetis). They sent for him, bound him, brought him, and consecrated him.
ኀበ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ አስቄጥስ ፡ ወፈነዉ ፡ ወአሰርዎ ፡ ወአብጽሕዎ ፡ ወሜምዎ ። ወሐለየ ፡ በአብያተ ፡ ክርስቲያናት ፤ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ወሐደሶሙ ፡ ወሐነጸ ፡ ዘተመዝ በረ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ወአእተተ ፡ በመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ግፅዞሙ ፡ ለዕልዋን ፡ እለ ፡ አስተርአዩ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ግብጽ ። ወገብሩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡፣ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያነ ፡ በባሕቲቶሙ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፤ ወገሠጾሙ ፣ ወሜጦሙ‹" ፡ ወጥለቆሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አባግዓ ፡ መርዔቱ ። ወከሠተ፤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዲበ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ብዙኃተ፣ ወፈወሶሙ ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወአውፅኦሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ እምላዕለ ፣ ሰብእ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ለአሐዱ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡፣ ለቡ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኢበጽሐከ ፤ ዘእንበለ ፡ 'እንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ትደፍር ፡ ወትነሥእ ፡ እምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ በእበድ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፤ ፍርሀት ፡ ወእምይእዜ ፡ ፅቀብ ፡ ነፍሰከ ፡ እምነገረ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እምአፉከ ። ወበመዋዕሊሁ ፡ ፄወዉ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ምዕራብ ፡ ተንበላት ፡'ለብዙኃን ፡ ሮም ፡ ክርስቲያን፣ወአብጽሕዎሙ ፡ ኀበ፡ሀገረ ፡እስክንድርያ ፡ወሜጥዎሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ 'ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ ሐዘነ ፡ ዓቢየ" ፡ ወተለቅሐ ፡ 'ላዕለ ፡ ጸባሕቱ" ፡ ወሰአለ ፡ እምነ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወተሣየጠ ፡ እምተንበላት ፡ ለብዙኃን ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሮም ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በ፻፻፻ዲናረ ፤ ወርቅ ። ወጸሐፈ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ወረሰዮሙ ፡ አግዓዝያነ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ እምኔክሙ ፡ከመ ፡ይሖር ፡-ኀበ ፡ ብሔሩ ፡አንሰ ፡እሁቦ ፡ስንቀ፣ ወእፌንዎ ። ወዘፈቀደ ፡፣ እምኔክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንበር ፡ በኀቤየ ፡ አነ ፣ እሴስዮ” ፡ ወአዓቅቦ ። ወቦእምኔሆሙ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ ወወሀቦሙ ፡ ስንቀ ፡ ወአስተፋነዎሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ። ወእለ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ በኀቤሁ ፡ አስተዋሰቦሙ ፡ ወዓቀቦሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሐለየ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡ በቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘመድኃኒነ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ እስክንድርያ ፡ ወሐደሳ ፤ ወአንሥአ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ሁከተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወተውፅየት ፡ በእሳት ። ወተመይጠ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አብ ፡'ወሐለየ ፡ ባቲ?" ፣ ወሐደሳ ፡ ካዕበ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፣፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያዕርፎ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወበዕለተ ፡ እሑድ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ፋሲካ ፣ አስተርአየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ወዜነዎ ፡ በእን፥ ፡ ትፍሥሕት ፡ ዘለዓለም ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ሎቱ ፤ እግዚአብሔር ። ወዓይድኦ ፡ ወይቤ,” ፡ እስመ ፤ እምድኅረ ፡ ተመጠውከ ፣ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡
During the time of his patriarchate, he took care of the churches, restored them, and rebuilt those among them that had been devastated. In his time also, he extirpated the doctrine of the heretics who had appeared in the land of Egypt. They had made a church for themselves alone and lived there. He reprimanded them, converted them, and counted them with the sheep of his flock. God manifested many miracles through the hands of this father: he healed many sick people and cast out unclean demons from people. He said to one of these: "Consider that this would not have happened to you if you had not had the audacity to receive the holy Mysteries with folly, without fear. Henceforth, guard yourself from the vain words that come out of your mouth." In his time, the Muslims of the West took many Christian people of Rom (= Rome) prisoner, brought them to the city of Alexandria, and sold them. This father felt great affliction because of this; he borrowed against his revenues, solicited the believers, and redeemed many Christian people of Rom from the Muslims for thirty thousand gold pieces. He wrote letters for them, set them free, and said to them: "Whoever among you wants to go to his country, I will give him travel expenses and send him back. Whoever among you wants to remain with me, I will feed and protect him." Some of them wanted to return to their country; he gave them travel expenses and had them escorted until they reached their country. Those who remained with him, he married them off and protected them. After this father had taken care of the church of our Savior which is in the city of Alexandria and had restored it, Satan stirred up a riot in the city and the church was burned by fire. This father took care of it again and restored it once more. When God willed that he should die, he was slightly ill. On the Sunday of the feast of Easter, the holy Märqos the Evangelist appeared to him and announced to him the eternal joy that God had prepared for him. He made it known to him and said to him: "After you have received the holy Mysteries,
